local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local function KickPlayer(reason)
    if Players.LocalPlayer then
        Players.LocalPlayer:Kick("\n\n🛡️ [ ALTHERIS SECURITY ] 🛡️\n\n⛔ Access Denied ⛔\nReason: " .. reason .. "\n\nPlease ensure you are using a supported executor and valid key.\n\n")
    end
    while true do task.wait() end
end

-- [RUNTIME SECURITY CHECKS]
local userId = Players.LocalPlayer and Players.LocalPlayer.UserId or 0
if userId == 0 then
    KickPlayer("Failed to verify User ID.")
end

-- Server Check Request
local req = request or http_request or (syn and syn.request)
if not req then
    KickPlayer("Executor does not support HTTP requests")
end

-- Anti Http-Spy: Dynamic Nonce
local nonce = math.random(1000000, 9999999)

local sessionToken = getgenv().SessionToken or "NO_TOKEN"
local verifyUrl = "https://altheris.xyz/v4/verify?session_token=" .. sessionToken .. "&userid=" .. tostring(userId) .. "&nonce=" .. tostring(nonce)

local response = req({
    Url = verifyUrl,
    Method = "GET"
})

-- Expected Formula from Server: (nonce * 3) + userId + 777
local expectedAuthKey = "ALTHERIS_AUTH:" .. tostring((nonce * 3) + userId + 777)

if not response or response.Body ~= expectedAuthKey then
    KickPlayer("Server Verification Failed or Spy Tool Detected. " .. tostring(response and response.Body or ""))
end

-- Custom Font Logic
local CustomFont = {}
function CustomFont:New(Name, Weight, Style, Data)
    pcall(function()
        if not isfile(Data.Id) then 
            writefile(Data.Id, game:HttpGet(Data.Url))
        end
    end)
    local asset = "rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json" -- Fallback
    pcall(function()
        asset = getcustomasset(Data.Id)
    end)
    return Font.new(asset, Enum.FontWeight[Weight], Enum.FontStyle[Style])
end

local CustomFontFace = Font.new(
    "rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json",
    Enum.FontWeight.Bold,
    Enum.FontStyle.Normal
)

-- LAZY LOAD LUCIDE ICONS
local LucideIcons = nil
local function FetchLucideIcons()
    if LucideIcons == nil then
        pcall(function()
            local iconData = game:HttpGet("https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Footagesus/Icons/refs/heads/main/lucide/dist/Icons.lua")
            if iconData then
                local loaded = loadstring(iconData)()
                if type(loaded) == "table" and type(loaded.assets) == "table" then
                    LucideIcons = loaded.assets
                else
                    LucideIcons = loaded
                end
            end
        end)
        if type(LucideIcons) ~= "table" then
            LucideIcons = {}
        end
    end
end

local function GetIcon(iconId)
    if type(iconId) ~= "string" then return iconId end
    if iconId:match("^rbxassetid://") then return iconId end
    if iconId:match("^rbxasset://") then return iconId end
    
    FetchLucideIcons()
    
   
    if LucideIcons[iconId] then return LucideIcons[iconId] end
    
    local prefixed = "lucide-" .. iconId
    if LucideIcons[prefixed] then return LucideIcons[prefixed] end
    
    return iconId 
end

function KLibrary:CreateWindow(Config)
    Config = Config or {}
    local WindowTitle = Config.Title or "System-Module"
    local WindowTheme = Config.Theme or "emerald"
    local IsPremiumUser = Config.IsPremium == nil and true or Config.IsPremium
    
    local BrandColor = Color3.fromRGB(180, 230, 255) 
    local BrandDim = Color3.fromRGB(90, 130, 160)
    
    if WindowTheme == "red" then
        BrandColor = Color3.fromRGB(225, 100, 100)
        BrandDim = Color3.fromRGB(120, 60, 60)
    elseif WindowTheme == "blue" then
        BrandColor = Color3.fromRGB(100, 150, 255)
        BrandDim = Color3.fromRGB(50, 80, 150)
    elseif WindowTheme == "purple" then
        BrandColor = Color3.fromRGB(180, 100, 255)
        BrandDim = Color3.fromRGB(90, 50, 130)
    elseif WindowTheme == "windsui" then
        BrandColor = Color3.fromRGB(125, 98, 255)
        BrandDim = Color3.fromRGB(146, 116, 255)
    end
    
    local DarkGrey = Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 50)

local BrandElements = {}

local function RegisterBrand(instance, property, colorType)
    table.insert(BrandElements, {Inst = instance, Prop = property, Type = colorType or "brand"})
end

local function ApplyTheme(smooth)
    local tweenInfo = smooth and TweenInfo.new(0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.Out) or nil
    for _, entry in ipairs(BrandElements) do
        if type(entry) == "function" then
            entry(smooth, tweenInfo, BrandColor, BrandDim)
        else
            local targetColor
            if entry.Type == "dim" then
                targetColor = BrandDim
            elseif entry.Type == "badge_bg" then
                targetColor = Color3.fromRGB(
                    math.floor(BrandColor.R * 255 * 0.15),
                    math.floor(BrandColor.G * 255 * 0.15),
                    math.floor(BrandColor.B * 255 * 0.15)
                )
            else
                targetColor = BrandColor
            end
            if smooth then
                TweenService:Create(entry.Inst, tweenInfo, {[entry.Prop] = targetColor}):Play()
            else
                entry.Inst[entry.Prop] = targetColor
            end
        end
    end
end

local fastSmooth = TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
local softPunch = TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
local pulseAnim = TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out, 0, true)

    else
        return PC_SIZE
    end
end

local function Notify(title, text, duration, notifType)
    duration = duration or 3
    notifType = notifType or "Info"
    local lowerTitle = string.lower(title)
    if lowerTitle:find("error") or lowerTitle:find("fail") or lowerTitle:find("invalid") or lowerTitle:find("denied") then notifType = "Error"
    elseif lowerTitle:find("success") or lowerTitle:find("done") or lowerTitle:find("copied") then notifType = "Success"
    elseif lowerTitle:find("warn") or lowerTitle:find("detect") then notifType = "Warning" end

    local iconId = "info"
    local iconColor = BrandColor
    if notifType == "Error" then 
        iconId = "x-circle"
        iconColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 80)
    elseif notifType == "Success" then
        iconId = "check-circle"
        iconColor = Color3.fromRGB(80, 255, 120)
    elseif notifType == "Warning" then
        iconId = "alert-triangle"
        iconColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 80)
    end
    
    local Wrapper = Instance.new("Frame")
    Wrapper.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 65)
    Wrapper.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    Wrapper.Parent = NotifyContainer
    
    local NotifFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
    NotifFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    NotifFrame.Position = UDim2.new(1, 20, 0, 0)
    NotifFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SectionAlt
    NotifFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    NotifFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    NotifFrame.ClipsDescendants = true
    NotifFrame.Parent = Wrapper
    
    local NotifCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    NotifCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    NotifCorner.Parent = NotifFrame

    local NotifStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    NotifStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 50)
    NotifStroke.Thickness = 1
    NotifStroke.Transparency = 1
    NotifStroke.Parent = NotifFrame
    
    local IconLbl = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    IconLbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 24, 0, 24)
    IconLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0.5, -12)
    IconLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    IconLbl.Image = GetIcon(iconId)
    IconLbl.ImageColor3 = iconColor
    IconLbl.ImageTransparency = 1
    IconLbl.Parent = NotifFrame

    local TitleLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    TitleLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -56, 0, 18)
    TitleLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 44, 0, 12)
    TitleLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    TitleLbl.Text = title
    TitleLbl.TextColor3 = iconColor
TitleLbl.FontFace = CustomFontFace
    TitleLbl.TextSize = 13
    TitleLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    TitleLbl.TextTransparency = 1
    TitleLbl.Parent = NotifFrame
    
    local DescLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    DescLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -56, 0, 18)
    DescLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 44, 0, 32)
    DescLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    DescLbl.Text = text
    DescLbl.TextColor3 = Theme.TextMuted
DescLbl.FontFace = CustomFontFace
    DescLbl.TextSize = 12
    DescLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    DescLbl.TextTransparency = 1
    DescLbl.TextWrapped = true
    DescLbl.Parent = NotifFrame

    local TimebarBg = Instance.new("Frame")
    TimebarBg.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 3)
    TimebarBg.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -3)
    TimebarBg.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Element
    TimebarBg.BorderSizePixel = 0
    TimebarBg.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    TimebarBg.Parent = NotifFrame

    local TimebarFill = Instance.new("Frame")
    TimebarFill.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    TimebarFill.BackgroundColor3 = iconColor
    TimebarFill.BorderSizePixel = 0
    TimebarFill.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    TimebarFill.Parent = TimebarBg

    if iconId == "info" then
        RegisterBrand(TitleLbl, "TextColor3", "brand")
        RegisterBrand(IconLbl, "ImageColor3", "brand")
        RegisterBrand(TimebarFill, "BackgroundColor3", "brand")
    end
    
    TweenService:Create(NotifFrame, TweenInfo.new(0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 0
    }):Play()
    TweenService:Create(NotifStroke, fastSmooth, {Transparency = 0}):Play()
    TweenService:Create(IconLbl, fastSmooth, {ImageTransparency = 0}):Play()
    TweenService:Create(TitleLbl, fastSmooth, {TextTransparency = 0}):Play()
    TweenService:Create(DescLbl, fastSmooth, {TextTransparency = 0}):Play()
    TweenService:Create(TimebarBg, fastSmooth, {BackgroundTransparency = 0}):Play()
    TweenService:Create(TimebarFill, fastSmooth, {BackgroundTransparency = 0}):Play()
    
    TweenService:Create(TimebarFill, TweenInfo.new(duration, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), {Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)}):Play()
    
    task.delay(duration, function()
        TweenService:Create(NotifFrame, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {
            Position = UDim2.new(1, 50, 0, 0),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1
        }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(NotifStroke, fastSmooth, {Transparency = 1}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(IconLbl, fastSmooth, {ImageTransparency = 1}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(TitleLbl, fastSmooth, {TextTransparency = 1}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(DescLbl, fastSmooth, {TextTransparency = 1}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(TimebarBg, fastSmooth, {BackgroundTransparency = 1}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(TimebarFill, fastSmooth, {BackgroundTransparency = 1}):Play()
        task.wait(0.3)
        Wrapper:Destroy()
    end)
end

    local objects = game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://" .. LogoDecalId)
    if objects and objects[1] then
        LogoImageId = objects[1].Texture or objects[1].Image or ""
    end
end)

end

-- SEARCH FILTER FUNCTION
SearchInput:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
    local query = string.lower(SearchInput.Text)
    local anyVisible = false

    for _, child in ipairs(TabScroller:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("Frame") then
            if query == "" then
                child.Visible = true
                anyVisible = true
            else
                local tabText = child:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel", true)
                local match = false
                if tabText and string.find(string.lower(tabText.Text), query, 1, true) then
                    match = true
                end
                -- Tìm trong sub-tabs
                for _, desc in ipairs(child:GetDescendants()) do
                    if desc:IsA("TextLabel") and string.find(string.lower(desc.Text), query, 1, true) then
                        match = true
                        break
                    end
                end
                child.Visible = match
                if match then anyVisible = true end
            end
        end
    end

    NoResultLabel.Visible = (query ~= "" and not anyVisible)
end)

FpsLayout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
FpsLayout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
FpsLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 4)
FpsLayout.Parent = FpsPill
local FpsIcon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
FpsIcon.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
FpsIcon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
FpsIcon.Image = GetIcon("gauge")
FpsIcon.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 160)
FpsIcon.Parent = FpsPill
local FpsText = Instance.new("TextLabel")
FpsText.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
FpsText.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
FpsText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
FpsText.Text = "0 FPS"
FpsText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 210)
FpsText.FontFace = CustomFontFace
FpsText.TextSize = 12
FpsText.Parent = FpsPill
local draggingToggle = false
local dragToggleInput, dragToggleStart, startTogglePos
ToggleUIBar.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        draggingToggle = true
        dragToggleStart = input.Position
        startTogglePos = ToggleUIBar.Position
        input.Changed:Connect(function()
            if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                draggingToggle = false
            end
        end)
    end
end)
ToggleUIBar.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        dragToggleInput = input
    end
end)
UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
    if input == dragToggleInput and draggingToggle then
        local delta = input.Position - dragToggleStart
        ToggleUIBar.Position = UDim2.new(startTogglePos.X.Scale, startTogglePos.X.Offset + delta.X, startTogglePos.Y.Scale, startTogglePos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
    end
end)

local isMenuOpen = true
local lastOpenPos = MainWindow.Position

local function getToggleSuckPos()
    -- Đọc vị trí trung tâm của ToggleUIBar, fallback về giữa màn hình nếu chưa render
    local absPos = ToggleUIBar.AbsolutePosition
    local absSize = ToggleUIBar.AbsoluteSize
    local cx = absPos.X + absSize.X / 2
    local cy = absPos.Y + absSize.Y / 2
    -- Nếu chưa render (= 0,0), dùng giữa viewport
    if cx == 0 and cy == 0 then
        local vp = workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize
        cx = vp.X / 2
        cy = vp.Y / 2
    end
    return UDim2.new(0, cx, 0, cy)
end

ToggleUIBar.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    -- Chống drag bị kích nhầm thành click
    if draggingToggle and (ToggleUIBar.AbsolutePosition ~= (startTogglePos and Vector2.new(startTogglePos.X.Offset, startTogglePos.Y.Offset) or ToggleUIBar.AbsolutePosition)) then
        return
    end

    isMenuOpen = not isMenuOpen

    if isMenuOpen then
        -- Hiện menu: đặt visible + scale về 0 trước, rồi tween lên
        MainWindow.Visible = true
        MainScale.Scale = 0
        MainWindow.Position = lastOpenPos
        TweenService:Create(MainScale, TweenInfo.new(0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Scale = 1}):Play()
    else
        -- Ẩn menu: tween scale về 0 rồi ẩn
        lastOpenPos = MainWindow.Position
        local suckPos = getToggleSuckPos()
        TweenService:Create(MainScale, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {Scale = 0}):Play()
        task.delay(0.25, function()
            if not isMenuOpen then
                MainWindow.Visible = false
                MainScale.Scale = 1 -- reset scale để lần sau mở không bị lỗi
            end
        end)
    end
end)

local function GetPing()
    local ping = 0
    pcall(function()
        ping = math.floor(Stats.Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue())
    end)
    return ping > 0 and ping or math.random(45, 55)
end

local function UpdateBottomBar()
    local totalTabs = #AllSubTabs
    BottomText.Text = string.format("Ready | %s | %d tabs | %d FPS | Key: Premium", currentActiveTab, totalTabs, currentFps)
end

RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    fpsCount = fpsCount + 1
end)

task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(1) do
        currentFps = fpsCount
        fpsCount = 0
        UpdateBottomBar()
        PingText.Text = string.format("%d ms", GetPing())
        FpsText.Text = string.format("%d FPS", currentFps)
    end
end)

local function CreateMainTab(tabName, iconAssetId)
    local CategoryFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
    CategoryFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
    CategoryFrame.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y 
    CategoryFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    CategoryFrame.Parent = TabScroller

    local CategoryLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    CategoryLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    CategoryLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 0)
    CategoryLayout.Parent = CategoryFrame

    local MainBtnContainer = Instance.new("Frame")
    MainBtnContainer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32)
    MainBtnContainer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    MainBtnContainer.LayoutOrder = 1
    MainBtnContainer.Parent = CategoryFrame

    local VisualBg = Instance.new("Frame")
    VisualBg.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, -2) 
    VisualBg.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 1)
    VisualBg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 45) 
    VisualBg.BackgroundTransparency = 1 
    VisualBg.BorderSizePixel = 0
    VisualBg.Parent = MainBtnContainer

    local VisualCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    VisualCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
    VisualCorner.Parent = VisualBg

    local BleedGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    BleedGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new{
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0),     
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.3, 0),   
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.85, 1),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
    }
    BleedGradient.Parent = VisualBg

    local VisualStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    VisualStroke.Thickness = 1
    VisualStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    VisualStroke.Transparency = 0.88
    VisualStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    VisualStroke.Parent = VisualBg

    local StrokeGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    StrokeGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new{
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 1),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
    }
    StrokeGradient.Parent = VisualStroke

    local ActiveSpine = Instance.new("Frame")
    ActiveSpine.Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 0)
    ActiveSpine.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, 0) 
    ActiveSpine.BackgroundColor3 = BrandColor 
    ActiveSpine.BorderSizePixel = 0
    ActiveSpine.BackgroundTransparency = 1 
    ActiveSpine.Parent = VisualBg
    RegisterBrand(ActiveSpine, "BackgroundColor3", "brand")
    
    local SpineCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    SpineCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    SpineCorner.Parent = ActiveSpine

    local TabBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    TabBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    TabBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1 
    TabBtn.Text = ""
    TabBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
    TabBtn.ZIndex = 5 
    TabBtn.Parent = VisualBg

    local MainIcon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    MainIcon.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
    MainIcon.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0.5, -7) 
    MainIcon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    MainIcon.Image = GetIcon(iconAssetId) or "rbxassetid://10734950309" 
    MainIcon.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(130, 130, 140) 
    MainIcon.ZIndex = 2
    MainIcon.Parent = VisualBg

    local TabText = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    TabText.Size = UDim2.new(1, -36, 1, 0)
    TabText.Position = UDim2.new(0, 34, 0, 0) 
    TabText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    TabText.Text = tabName
    TabText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(130, 130, 140)
    TabText.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    TabText.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.8
TabText.FontFace = CustomFontFace
    TabText.TextSize = math.floor(12 * TextScale)
    TabText.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    TabText.ZIndex = 2
    TabText.Parent = VisualBg

    local SubContainer = Instance.new("Frame")
    SubContainer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0) 
    SubContainer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    SubContainer.BorderSizePixel = 0
    SubContainer.ClipsDescendants = true 
    SubContainer.Visible = false 
    SubContainer.LayoutOrder = 2
    SubContainer.Parent = CategoryFrame

    local SubListLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    SubListLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    SubListLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 3) -- Khoảng cách giữa các sub-tab
    SubListLayout.Parent = SubContainer

    local mainRecord = { Visual = VisualBg, Spine = ActiveSpine, Text = TabText, Icon = MainIcon, Container = SubContainer, SubTabsList = {}, IsOpen = false }
    table.insert(AllMainTabs, mainRecord)

    TabBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        if not mainRecord.IsOpen then
            TweenService:Create(VisualBg, fastSmooth, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.85}):Play()
            TweenService:Create(TabText, fastSmooth, {TextColor3 = Theme.TextMuted}):Play()
        end
    end)
    TabBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        if not mainRecord.IsOpen then
            TweenService:Create(VisualBg, fastSmooth, {BackgroundTransparency = 1}):Play()
            TweenService:Create(TabText, fastSmooth, {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(130, 130, 140)}):Play()
        end
    end)

    TabBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        mainRecord.IsOpen = not mainRecord.IsOpen
        local targetHeight = #mainRecord.SubTabsList * 30 + (#mainRecord.SubTabsList - 1) * 3

        if mainRecord.IsOpen then
            TweenService:Create(VisualBg, fastSmooth, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.6}):Play() 
            TweenService:Create(ActiveSpine, fastSmooth, {Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 16), Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, -8), BackgroundTransparency = 0}):Play() 
            TweenService:Create(MainIcon, fastSmooth, {ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)}):Play() 
            TweenService:Create(TabText, fastSmooth, {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)}):Play()
            mainRecord.Text.FontFace = CustomFontFace
            
            SubContainer.Visible = true
            TweenService:Create(SubContainer, fastSmooth, {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, targetHeight)}):Play()
        else
            TweenService:Create(VisualBg, fastSmooth, {BackgroundTransparency = 1}):Play()
            TweenService:Create(ActiveSpine, fastSmooth, {Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1}):Play() 
            TweenService:Create(MainIcon, fastSmooth, {ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(130, 130, 140)}):Play()
            TweenService:Create(TabText, fastSmooth, {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(130, 130, 140)}):Play()
            mainRecord.Text.FontFace = CustomFontFace
            
            local closeTween = TweenService:Create(SubContainer, fastSmooth, {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)})
            closeTween:Play()
            closeTween.Completed:Connect(function()
                if not mainRecord.IsOpen then SubContainer.Visible = false end
            end)
        end
    end)

    local function AddSubTab(subName, subDesc, iconAssetId)
        subDesc = subDesc or ""
        local order = #mainRecord.SubTabsList + 1

        local PageFrame = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
        PageFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)

        PageFrame.MidImage = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Scroll/scroll-middle.png"
        PageFrame.BottomImage = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Scroll/scroll-middle.png"
        PageFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        PageFrame.Visible = false
        PageFrame.Parent = ContentArea

        local LeftColumn = Instance.new("Frame")
        LeftColumn.Name = "LeftColumn"
        LeftColumn.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -5, 0, 0)
        LeftColumn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        LeftColumn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        LeftColumn.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        LeftColumn.Parent = PageFrame

        local LeftLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        LeftLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        LeftLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 10)
        LeftLayout.Parent = LeftColumn

        local RightColumn = Instance.new("Frame")
        RightColumn.Name = "RightColumn"
        RightColumn.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -5, 0, 0)
        RightColumn.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 5, 0, 0)
        RightColumn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        RightColumn.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        RightColumn.Parent = PageFrame

        local RightLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        RightLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        RightLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 10)
        RightLayout.Parent = RightColumn

        local ContentPadding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
        ContentPadding.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 15)
        ContentPadding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 15)
        ContentPadding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 15)
        ContentPadding.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 15)
        ContentPadding.Parent = PageFrame

         -- [CHÈN ĐOẠN CẢM BIẾN NÀY VÀO ĐÂY]
        local function updateCanvas()
            task.wait() -- Đợi UI render xong các nút
            local leftHeight = LeftColumn.AbsoluteSize.Y
            local rightHeight = RightColumn.AbsoluteSize.Y
            -- Tính xem cột nào dài hơn, lấy cột đó làm chuẩn và cộng thêm 20px lề đáy
            local maxHeight = math.max(leftHeight, rightHeight)
            PageFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, maxHeight + 20)
        end
        
        -- Gắn cảm biến: Cứ hễ chèn thêm/xóa bớt nút ở 2 cột là tự động chạy hàm cập nhật
        LeftColumn:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(updateCanvas)
        RightColumn:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(updateCanvas)
        -- [KẾT THÚC CHÈN]

        table.insert(AllPageFrames, PageFrame)

        local SubBtnContainer = Instance.new("Frame")
        SubBtnContainer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30)
        SubBtnContainer.BackgroundTransparency = 1 
        SubBtnContainer.LayoutOrder = order
        SubBtnContainer.Parent = SubContainer

        -- ĐƯỜNG KẺ PHÂN CÁCH MẢNH NHƯ TƠ giữa các tab (ẩn ở tab đầu)
        if order > 1 then
            local SepLine = Instance.new("Frame")
            SepLine.Size = UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 1)
            SepLine.Position = UDim2.new(0, 25, 0, 0)

            SepLine.ZIndex = 1

            local SepGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient")

                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.2, 0.9),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0.85),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.8, 0.9),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
            }

        end

        -- NỀN PILL NÂNG CẤP
        local PillBg = Instance.new("Frame")
        PillBg.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 1, -4)
        PillBg.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 2)

Chevron.FontFace = CustomFontFace

        PillStroke.Thickness = 1

        table.insert(BrandElements, function(smooth, tweenInfo, brandColor, brandDim)
            if currentActiveTab == subName then
                if smooth and tweenInfo then
                    TweenService:Create(Chevron, tweenInfo, {TextColor3 = brandColor}):Play()

                    Chevron.TextColor3 = brandColor
