        }
        TextGradient.Rotation = 0
        TextGradient.Offset = Vector2.new(-2, 0) -- Giấu gradient ra ngoài
        TextGradient.Parent = SubText

        -- MŨI TÊN PHẢI (trượt vào khi active)
        local RightArrow = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        RightArrow.Size = UDim2.new(0, 12, 1, 0)
        RightArrow.Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0, 0) 
        RightArrow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        RightArrow.Text = "›"
        RightArrow.TextColor3 = BrandColor
        RightArrow.TextTransparency = 1
RightArrow.FontFace = CustomFontFace
        RightArrow.TextSize = math.floor(14 * TextScale)
        RightArrow.ZIndex = 3
        RightArrow.Parent = PillBg
        RegisterBrand(RightArrow, "TextColor3", "brand")

        -- SHIMMER GRADIENT
        local ShimmerOverlay = Instance.new("Frame")
        ShimmerOverlay.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        ShimmerOverlay.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        ShimmerOverlay.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        ShimmerOverlay.BorderSizePixel = 0
        ShimmerOverlay.ZIndex = 2
        ShimmerOverlay.Parent = PillBg

        local ShimmerCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        ShimmerCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
        ShimmerCorner.Parent = ShimmerOverlay

        local ShimmerGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        ShimmerGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new{
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, BrandColor),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.6, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255))
        }
        ShimmerGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new{
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4, 1),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0.85),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.6, 1),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
        }
        ShimmerGradient.Offset = Vector2.new(-1, 0)
        ShimmerGradient.Rotation = 25
        ShimmerGradient.Parent = ShimmerOverlay

        local SubBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        SubBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0) 
        SubBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1 
        SubBtn.Text = ""
        SubBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        SubBtn.ZIndex = 5
        SubBtn.Parent = SubBtnContainer

        local subData = {
            Pill = PillBg, Text = SubText, Accent = AccentBar, Stroke = PillStroke,
            Chevron = Chevron, Shimmer = ShimmerOverlay, ShimmerGrad = ShimmerGradient,
            Arrow = RightArrow, TextGrad = TextGradient,
            Order = order, PageFrame = PageFrame
        }
        table.insert(mainRecord.SubTabsList, subData)
        table.insert(AllSubTabs, subData)

        SubBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            if SubText.FontFace ~= CustomFontFace then
                TweenService:Create(PillBg, fastSmooth, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.88}):Play()
                TweenService:Create(SubText, fastSmooth, {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 210)}):Play()
                TweenService:Create(Chevron, fastSmooth, {TextColor3 = Theme.TextDark, Rotation = 15}):Play()
            end
        end)
        SubBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            if SubText.FontFace ~= CustomFontFace then
                TweenService:Create(PillBg, fastSmooth, {BackgroundTransparency = 1}):Play()
                TweenService:Create(SubText, fastSmooth, {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(110, 110, 120)}):Play()
                TweenService:Create(Chevron, fastSmooth, {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 68), Rotation = 0}):Play()
            end
        end)

        SubBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            -- Reset tất cả sub tabs
            for _, s in pairs(AllSubTabs) do
                TweenService:Create(s.Pill, fastSmooth, {BackgroundTransparency = 1}):Play()
                TweenService:Create(s.Stroke, fastSmooth, {Transparency = 1}):Play()
                TweenService:Create(s.Accent, fastSmooth, {Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), ImageTransparency = 1}):Play()
                TweenService:Create(s.Chevron, fastSmooth, {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 68), Rotation = 0}):Play()
                TweenService:Create(s.Shimmer, fastSmooth, {BackgroundTransparency = 1}):Play()
                TweenService:Create(s.Text, fastSmooth, {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(110, 110, 120)}):Play()
                TweenService:Create(s.Arrow, fastSmooth, {TextTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0, 0)}):Play()
                s.TextGrad.Offset = Vector2.new(-2, 0)
                s.Text.FontFace = CustomFontFace
            end

            -- Kích hoạt tab được chọn
            currentActiveTab = subName
            UpdateBottomBar()
            for _, s in pairs(mainRecord.SubTabsList) do
                if s.Order == order then
                    -- Nền Pill tinh tế với gradient xịn
                    TweenService:Create(s.Pill, fastSmooth, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.85}):Play()
                    -- Hiện viền màu
                    TweenService:Create(s.Stroke, fastSmooth, {Transparency = 0.4}):Play()
                    -- Icon premium trượt từ góc phải ra
                    TweenService:Create(s.Accent, TweenInfo.new(0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Elastic, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14), ImageTransparency = 0}):Play()
                    -- Chevron xoay + đổi màu xanh
                    TweenService:Create(s.Chevron, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {TextColor3 = BrandColor, Rotation = 90}):Play()
                    -- Text trắng sáng
                    TweenService:Create(s.Text, fastSmooth, {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)}):Play()
                    s.Text.FontFace = CustomFontFace
                    -- Mũi tên phải trượt vào
                    TweenService:Create(s.Arrow, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {TextTransparency = 0.3, Position = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 0)}):Play()
                    -- Shimmer chạy 1 lần
                    s.ShimmerGrad.Offset = Vector2.new(-1, 0)
                    TweenService:Create(s.ShimmerGrad, TweenInfo.new(0.8, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {Offset = Vector2.new(1, 0)}):Play()
                    -- Text gradient metallic chạy lặp
                    task.spawn(function()
                        task.wait(0.3)
                        s.TextGrad.Offset = Vector2.new(-2, 0)
                        TweenService:Create(s.TextGrad, TweenInfo.new(1.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {Offset = Vector2.new(2, 0)}):Play()
                    end)
                end
            end
        end)

        -- Cập nhật Content Header + Show Page khi click
        SubBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            ContentTitle.Text = subName
            ContentDesc.Text = subDesc
            
            for _, page in ipairs(AllPageFrames) do
                page.Visible = false
            end
            PageFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 0)
            PageFrame.Visible = true
            TweenService:Create(PageFrame, fastSmooth, {Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)}):Play()
        end)
        
        return PageFrame
    end

    return { AddSubTab = AddSubTab }
end

-- ==========================================================
-- THƯ VIỆN UI ELEMENTS
-- ==========================================================

-- [SECTION HEADER]
local function CreateSection(pageFrame, title, side, layoutOrder)
    local targetColumn = (side == "Right") and pageFrame:FindFirstChild("RightColumn") or pageFrame:FindFirstChild("LeftColumn")

    local SectionFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
    SectionFrame.Name = "SectionFrame"
    SectionFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
    SectionFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(24, 24, 31)
    SectionFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    
    local SectionStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    SectionStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(75, 75, 90) -- Làm viền sáng hơn để hiện rõ
    SectionStroke.Transparency = 0.2 -- Giảm độ trong suốt để viền rõ nét
    SectionStroke.Thickness = 1
    SectionStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    SectionStroke.Parent = SectionFrame
    SectionFrame.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    SectionFrame.LayoutOrder = layoutOrder or 0
    SectionFrame.Parent = targetColumn

    local SectionCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    SectionCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
    SectionCorner.Parent = SectionFrame

    -- Accent Bar tự động tỷ lệ theo chữ
    local accentHeight = math.floor(14 * TextScale)
    local accentY = 5 + (30 - accentHeight) / 2
    local AccentBar = Instance.new("Frame")
    AccentBar.Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, accentHeight)
    AccentBar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, accentY)
    AccentBar.BackgroundColor3 = BrandColor
    AccentBar.BorderSizePixel = 0
    AccentBar.Parent = SectionFrame
    RegisterBrand(AccentBar, "BackgroundColor3", "normal")

    local AccentCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    AccentCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    AccentCorner.Parent = AccentBar

    local AccentGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    AccentGradient.Rotation = 90
    AccentGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.5),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.2, 0),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.8, 0),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.5)
    })
    AccentGradient.Parent = AccentBar

    local Title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    Title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 30)
    Title.Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 5)
    Title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    Title.Text = title
    Title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 225)
    Title.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    Title.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.8
Title.FontFace = CustomFontFace
    Title.TextSize = math.floor(14 * TextScale)
    Title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    Title.Parent = SectionFrame

    -- Thanh ngang mờ dần (Gradient Separator)
    local SectionSeparator = Instance.new("Frame")
    SectionSeparator.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 1)
    SectionSeparator.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 32)
    SectionSeparator.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 90)
    SectionSeparator.BorderSizePixel = 0
    SectionSeparator.Parent = SectionFrame

    local SepGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    SepGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.6, 0.8),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
    })
    SepGradient.Parent = SectionSeparator

    local ContentContainer = Instance.new("Frame")
    ContentContainer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
    ContentContainer.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 35)
    ContentContainer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    ContentContainer.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    ContentContainer.Parent = SectionFrame

    local ContentLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    ContentLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    ContentLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
    ContentLayout.Parent = ContentContainer

    local ContentPadding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
    ContentPadding.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 0)
    ContentPadding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 10)
    ContentPadding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 10)
    ContentPadding.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 15)
    ContentPadding.Parent = ContentContainer

    return ContentContainer
end

-- [TEXTBOX]
local function CreateTextBox(parent, title, placeholder, clearOnFocus, callback, layoutOrder)
    local Row = Instance.new("Frame")
    Row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 50)
    Row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    Row.BorderSizePixel = 0
    Row.LayoutOrder = layoutOrder or 0
    Row.Parent = parent

    local Label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    Label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 16)
    Label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 2)
    Label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    Label.Text = title
    Label.TextColor3 = Theme.TextMuted
    Label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    Label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.8
Label.FontFace = CustomFontFace
    Label.TextSize = math.floor(12.5 * TextScale)
    Label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    Label.Parent = Row

    local BoxFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
    BoxFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 28)
    BoxFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 20)
    BoxFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(38, 35, 56) -- WindsUI bgRowSel
    BoxFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    BoxFrame.Parent = Row

    local BoxCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    BoxCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
    BoxCorner.Parent = BoxFrame

    local BoxStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    BoxStroke.Thickness = 1.5
    BoxStroke.Color = BrandColor
    BoxStroke.Transparency = 1 -- Ẩn viền khi chưa focus
    BoxStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    BoxStroke.Parent = BoxFrame
    RegisterBrand(BoxStroke, "Color", "brand")

    local Input = Instance.new("TextBox")
    Input.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, 0)
    Input.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0)
    Input.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    Input.Text = ""
    Input.PlaceholderText = placeholder or "Enter text..."
    Input.PlaceholderColor3 = Theme.TextDark
    Input.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
Input.FontFace = CustomFontFace
    Input.TextSize = math.floor(11.5 * TextScale)
    Input.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    Input.ClearTextOnFocus = clearOnFocus == nil and false or clearOnFocus
    Input.Parent = BoxFrame

    -- Animation khi click vào TextBox (Focus)
    Input.Focused:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(BoxFrame, fastSmooth, {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.ElementHover}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(BoxStroke, fastSmooth, {Transparency = 0}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(Label, fastSmooth, {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)}):Play()
    end)

    -- Animation khi click ra ngoài hoặc nhấn Enter (Focus Lost)
    Input.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
        TweenService:Create(BoxFrame, fastSmooth, {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Element}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(BoxStroke, fastSmooth, {Transparency = 1}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(Label, fastSmooth, {TextColor3 = Theme.TextMuted}):Play()
        
        if callback then
            callback(Input.Text, enterPressed)
        end
    end)

    return Row
end

-- [TOGGLE]
local function CreateToggle(parent, title, default, callback, layoutOrder)
    local isOn = default or false

    local Row = Instance.new("Frame")
    Row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30)
    Row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    Row.BorderSizePixel = 0
    Row.LayoutOrder = layoutOrder or 0
    Row.Parent = parent

    local Label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    Label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -50, 1, 0)
    Label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0)
    Label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    Label.Text = title
    Label.TextColor3 = isOn and Theme.Text or Theme.TextMuted
    Label.FontFace = CustomFontFace
    Label.TextSize = math.floor(12.5 * TextScale)
    Label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    Label.Parent = Row

    -- Library.lua Style Large Toggle Switch (36x20)
    local ToggleBg = Instance.new("Frame")
    ToggleBg.Size = UDim2.new(0, 36, 0, 20)
    ToggleBg.Position = UDim2.new(1, -48, 0.5, -10)
    ToggleBg.BackgroundColor3 = isOn and BrandColor or Color3.fromRGB(58, 58, 72)
    ToggleBg.BorderSizePixel = 0
    ToggleBg.Parent = Row

    local ToggleBgCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    ToggleBgCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    ToggleBgCorner.Parent = ToggleBg

    local UIStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    UIStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    UIStroke.Transparency = 0.5
    UIStroke.Parent = ToggleBg

    local Knob = Instance.new("Frame")
    Knob.Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16)
    Knob.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
    Knob.Position = isOn and UDim2.new(0, 18, 0.5, 0) or UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, 0)
    Knob.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    Knob.BorderSizePixel = 0
    Knob.Parent = ToggleBg

    local KnobCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    KnobCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    KnobCorner.Parent = Knob

    local KnobGlow = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    KnobGlow.Name = "Glow"
    KnobGlow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    KnobGlow.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
    KnobGlow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 20, 1, 20)
    KnobGlow.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    KnobGlow.Image = "rbxassetid://18245826428"
    KnobGlow.ImageColor3 = BrandColor
    KnobGlow.ImageTransparency = isOn and 0.7 or 1
    KnobGlow.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice
    KnobGlow.SliceCenter = Rect.new(21, 21, 79, 79)
    KnobGlow.ZIndex = 2
    KnobGlow.Parent = Knob
    RegisterBrand(KnobGlow, "ImageColor3", "brand")

    local Btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    Btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    Btn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    Btn.Text = ""
    Btn.ZIndex = 5
    Btn.Parent = Row

    Btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        isOn = not isOn
        TweenService:Create(ToggleBg, fastSmooth, {
            BackgroundColor3 = isOn and BrandColor or Color3.fromRGB(58, 58, 72)
        }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(Knob, fastSmooth, {
            Position = isOn and UDim2.new(0, 18, 0.5, 0) or UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, 0)
        }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(KnobGlow, fastSmooth, {
            ImageTransparency = isOn and 0.7 or 1
        }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(Label, fastSmooth, {
            TextColor3 = isOn and Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 248) or Theme.TextMuted
        }):Play()
        
        if Notify then
            Notify("Toggle", title .. " has been turned " .. (isOn and "ON" or "OFF"), 3)
        end
        
        if callback then callback(isOn) end
    end)
    table.insert(BrandElements, function(smooth, tweenInfo, bColor, bDim)
        if isOn then
            if smooth then
                TweenService:Create(ToggleBg, tweenInfo, {BackgroundColor3 = bColor}):Play()
            else
                ToggleBg.BackgroundColor3 = bColor
            end
        end
    end)
    local API = {}
    function API:Set(value)
        isOn = value
        TweenService:Create(ToggleBg, fastSmooth, {
            BackgroundColor3 = isOn and BrandColor or Color3.fromRGB(58, 58, 72)
        }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(Knob, fastSmooth, {
            Position = isOn and UDim2.new(0, 18, 0.5, 0) or UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, 0)
        }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(KnobGlow, fastSmooth, {
            ImageTransparency = isOn and 0.7 or 1
        }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(Label, fastSmooth, {
            TextColor3 = isOn and Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 248) or Theme.TextMuted
        }):Play()
        if callback then callback(isOn) end
    end
    return API
end

-- [LABEL]
local function CreateLabel(parent, text, layoutOrder)
    local Row = Instance.new("Frame")
    Row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24)
    Row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    Row.BorderSizePixel = 0
    Row.LayoutOrder = layoutOrder or 0
    Row.Parent = parent

    local Label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    Label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, 0)
    Label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0)
    Label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    Label.Text = text
    Label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 210)
Label.FontFace = CustomFontFace
    Label.TextSize = math.floor(12 * TextScale)
    Label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    Label.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
    Label.Parent = Row
    
    return Label
end

-- [BUTTON]
local function CreateButton(parent, title, callback, layoutOrder)
    local Row = Instance.new("Frame")
    Row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30)
    Row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    Row.BorderSizePixel = 0
    Row.LayoutOrder = layoutOrder or 0
    Row.Parent = parent

    local Bar = Instance.new("Frame")
    Bar.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 26)
    Bar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0.5, -13)
    Bar.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Element
    Bar.BorderSizePixel = 0
    Bar.Parent = Row

    local BarCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    BarCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
    BarCorner.Parent = Bar

    local UIStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    UIStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    UIStroke.Transparency = 0.5
    UIStroke.Parent = Bar

    local Label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    Label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 1, 0)
    Label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0)
    Label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    Label.Text = title
    Label.TextColor3 = Theme.TextMuted
    Label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    Label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.8
    Label.FontFace = CustomFontFace
    Label.TextSize = math.floor(12.5 * TextScale)
    Label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    Label.Parent = Bar

    local MouseIcon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    MouseIcon.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
    MouseIcon.Position = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0.5, -7)
    MouseIcon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    MouseIcon.Image = "rbxassetid://18228308832" -- Mouse pointer icon
    MouseIcon.ImageColor3 = Theme.TextMuted
    MouseIcon.Parent = Bar

    local Btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    Btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    Btn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    Btn.Text = ""
    Btn.ZIndex = 5
    Btn.Parent = Bar

    Btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(Bar, fastSmooth, {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.ElementHover}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(Label, fastSmooth, {TextColor3 = Theme.Text}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(MouseIcon, fastSmooth, {ImageColor3 = BrandColor}):Play()
    end)
    Btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(Bar, fastSmooth, {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Element}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(Label, fastSmooth, {TextColor3 = Theme.TextMuted}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(MouseIcon, fastSmooth, {ImageColor3 = Theme.TextMuted}):Play()
    end)
    Btn.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(Bar, fastSmooth, {BackgroundColor3 = BrandColor}):Play()
    end)
    Btn.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(Bar, fastSmooth, {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.ElementHover}):Play()
    end)
    Btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if Notify then Notify("Action", title, 2) end
        if callback then callback() end
    end)
    return Row
end

-- [SLIDER]
local function CreateSlider(parent, title, min, max, default, callback, layoutOrder)
    local value = default or min

    local Row = Instance.new("Frame")
    Row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 45)
    Row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    Row.BorderSizePixel = 0
    Row.LayoutOrder = layoutOrder or 0
    Row.Parent = parent

    local Label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    Label.Size = UDim2.new(0.7, -10, 0, 18)
    Label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 5)
    Label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    Label.Text = title
    Label.TextColor3 = Theme.TextMuted
    Label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    Label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.8
Label.FontFace = CustomFontFace
    Label.TextSize = math.floor(12.5 * TextScale)
    Label.TextScaled = true
    Label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    Label.Parent = Row

    local LabelConstraint = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint")
    LabelConstraint.MaxTextSize = math.floor(12.5 * TextScale)
    LabelConstraint.Parent = Label

    local ValueInput = Instance.new("TextBox")
    ValueInput.Size = UDim2.new(0.3, -14, 0, 18)
    ValueInput.Position = UDim2.new(0.7, 0, 0, 5)
    ValueInput.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    ValueInput.Text = tostring(value)
    ValueInput.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(120, 120, 130)
    ValueInput.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    ValueInput.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.8
ValueInput.FontFace = CustomFontFace
    ValueInput.TextSize = math.floor(12.5 * TextScale)
    ValueInput.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
    ValueInput.ClearTextOnFocus = false
    ValueInput.Parent = Row

    local SliderTrack = Instance.new("Frame")
    SliderTrack.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 6)
    SliderTrack.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 32)
    SliderTrack.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Element
    SliderTrack.BorderSizePixel = 0
    SliderTrack.Parent = Row

    local SliderTrackCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    SliderTrackCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    SliderTrackCorner.Parent = SliderTrack

    local SliderTrackStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    SliderTrackStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    SliderTrackStroke.Transparency = 0.5
    SliderTrackStroke.Parent = SliderTrack

    local pct = (value - min) / (max - min)
    local SliderFill = Instance.new("Frame")
    SliderFill.Size = UDim2.new(pct, 0, 1, 0)
    SliderFill.BackgroundColor3 = BrandColor
    SliderFill.BorderSizePixel = 0
    SliderFill.Parent = SliderTrack

    local SliderFillCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    SliderFillCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    SliderFillCorner.Parent = SliderFill
    RegisterBrand(SliderFill, "BackgroundColor3", "brand")

    local SliderFillGlow = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    SliderFillGlow.Name = "Glow"
    SliderFillGlow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    SliderFillGlow.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
    SliderFillGlow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 25, 1, 25)
    SliderFillGlow.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    SliderFillGlow.Image = "rbxassetid://18245826428"
    SliderFillGlow.ImageColor3 = BrandColor
    SliderFillGlow.ImageTransparency = 0.8
    SliderFillGlow.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice
    SliderFillGlow.SliceCenter = Rect.new(21, 21, 79, 79)
    SliderFillGlow.ZIndex = 2
    SliderFillGlow.Parent = SliderFill
    RegisterBrand(SliderFillGlow, "ImageColor3", "brand")

    local SliderKnob = Instance.new("Frame")
    SliderKnob.Size = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 12)
    SliderKnob.Position = UDim2.new(pct, -6, 0.5, -6)
    SliderKnob.BackgroundColor3 = BrandColor
    SliderKnob.BorderSizePixel = 0
    SliderKnob.ZIndex = 3
    SliderKnob.Parent = SliderTrack
    RegisterBrand(SliderKnob, "BackgroundColor3", "brand")

    local SliderKnobCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    SliderKnobCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    SliderKnobCorner.Parent = SliderKnob

    local SliderKnobGlow = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    SliderKnobGlow.Name = "Glow"
    SliderKnobGlow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    SliderKnobGlow.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
    SliderKnobGlow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 25, 1, 25)
    SliderKnobGlow.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    SliderKnobGlow.Image = "rbxassetid://18245826428"
    SliderKnobGlow.ImageColor3 = BrandColor
    SliderKnobGlow.ImageTransparency = 0.8
    SliderKnobGlow.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice
    SliderKnobGlow.SliceCenter = Rect.new(21, 21, 79, 79)
    SliderKnobGlow.ZIndex = 2
    SliderKnobGlow.Parent = SliderKnob
    RegisterBrand(SliderKnobGlow, "ImageColor3", "brand")

    local draggingSlider = false
    local SliderBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    SliderBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24)
    SliderBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 24)
    SliderBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    SliderBtn.Text = ""
    SliderBtn.ZIndex = 5
    SliderBtn.Parent = Row

    local Scale = Instance.new("UIScale")
    Scale.Parent = Row

    local function updateSlider(input)
        local trackAbsPos = SliderTrack.AbsolutePosition.X
        local trackAbsSize = SliderTrack.AbsoluteSize.X
        local mouseX = input.Position.X
        local newPct = math.clamp((mouseX - trackAbsPos) / trackAbsSize, 0, 1)
        
        local isFloat = (type(min) == "number" and math.floor(min) ~= min) or (type(max) == "number" and math.floor(max) ~= max) or (type(default) == "number" and math.floor(default) ~= default)
        local newVal
        if isFloat then
            newVal = min + (max - min) * newPct
            -- Làm tròn 2 chữ số thập phân
            newVal = math.floor(newVal * 100 + 0.5) / 100
        else
            newVal = math.floor(min + (max - min) * newPct + 0.5)
        end
        value = newVal
        local realPct = (newVal - min) / (max - min)
        local sliderSpeed = TweenInfo.new(0.06, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
        TweenService:Create(SliderFill, sliderSpeed, {Size = UDim2.new(realPct, 0, 1, 0)}):Play()
        local offset = draggingSlider and -8 or -6
        local knobSize = draggingSlider and 16 or 12
        TweenService:Create(SliderKnob, sliderSpeed, {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, knobSize, 0, knobSize),
            Position = UDim2.new(realPct, offset, 0.5, offset)
        }):Play()
        
        ValueInput.Text = tostring(newVal)
        if callback then callback(newVal) end
    end

    ValueInput.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
        local inputVal = tonumber(ValueInput.Text)
        if inputVal then
            local isFloat = (type(min) == "number" and math.floor(min) ~= min) or (type(max) == "number" and math.floor(max) ~= max) or (type(default) == "number" and math.floor(default) ~= default)
            if isFloat then
                inputVal = math.floor(inputVal * 100 + 0.5) / 100
            else
                inputVal = math.floor(inputVal + 0.5)
            end
            inputVal = math.clamp(inputVal, min, max)
            value = inputVal
            
            local realPct = (value - min) / (max - min)
            TweenService:Create(SliderFill, fastSmooth, {Size = UDim2.new(realPct, 0, 1, 0)}):Play()
            TweenService:Create(SliderKnob, fastSmooth, {Position = UDim2.new(realPct, -7, 0.5, -7)}):Play()
            
            ValueInput.Text = tostring(value)
            if Notify then Notify("Value Updated", title .. ": " .. tostring(value), 2) end
            if callback then callback(value) end
        else
            ValueInput.Text = tostring(value)
        end
    end)

    SliderBtn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            draggingSlider = true
            TweenService:Create(Scale, fastSmooth, {Scale = 0.98}):Play()
            TweenService:Create(SliderKnob, fastSmooth, {Size = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 18), BackgroundColor3 = BrandColor}):Play()
            updateSlider(input)
        end
    end)

    UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if draggingSlider and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            updateSlider(input)
        end
    end)

    UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            if draggingSlider then
                draggingSlider = false
                TweenService:Create(Scale, fastSmooth, {Scale = 1}):Play()
                TweenService:Create(SliderKnob, fastSmooth, {Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14), BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Text}):Play()
                local realPct = (value - min) / (max - min)
                TweenService:Create(SliderKnob, fastSmooth, {Position = UDim2.new(realPct, -7, 0.5, -7)}):Play()
                if Notify then Notify("Slider Updated", title .. ": " .. tostring(value), 2) end
            end
        end
    end)
    local API = {}
    function API:Set(newVal)
        local val = math.clamp(newVal, min, max)
        value = val
        local pct = (val - min) / (max - min)
        SliderFill.Size = UDim2.new(pct, 0, 1, 0)
        SliderKnob.Position = UDim2.new(pct, -7, 0.5, -7)
        ValueInput.Text = tostring(val)
        if callback then callback(val) end
    end
    return API
end

-- [DROPDOWN]
local function CreateDropdown(parent, title, options, default, callback, layoutOrder)
    local selected = default or options[1]

    local Row = Instance.new("Frame")
    Row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30)
    Row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    Row.BorderSizePixel = 0
    Row.LayoutOrder = layoutOrder or 0
    Row.Parent = parent

    -- Label ben trai
    local Label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    Label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -110, 1, 0)
    Label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0)
    Label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    Label.Text = title
    Label.TextColor3 = Theme.TextMuted
    Label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    Label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.8
    Label.FontFace = CustomFontFace
    Label.TextSize = math.floor(12.5 * TextScale)
    Label.TextScaled = true
    Label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    Label.Parent = Row

    local LabelConstraint = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint")
    LabelConstraint.MaxTextSize = math.floor(12.5 * TextScale)
    LabelConstraint.Parent = Label

    -- Box rộng thêm 1 chút (86px) để chứa chữ dài
    local BoxFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
    BoxFrame.Name = "Dropdown_BoxFrame"
    BoxFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 86, 0, 24)
    BoxFrame.Position = UDim2.new(1, -98, 0.5, -12)
    BoxFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Element
    BoxFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    BoxFrame.Parent = Row

    local BoxCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    BoxCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
    BoxCorner.Parent = BoxFrame

    local BoxStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    BoxStroke.Thickness = 1.5
    BoxStroke.Color = Theme.Outline
    BoxStroke.Transparency = 0
    BoxStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    BoxStroke.Parent = BoxFrame

    local BoxGlow = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    BoxGlow.Name = "Glow"
    BoxGlow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    BoxGlow.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
    BoxGlow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 25, 1, 25)
    BoxGlow.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    BoxGlow.Image = "rbxassetid://18245826428"
    BoxGlow.ImageColor3 = BrandColor
    BoxGlow.ImageTransparency = 1 -- Only visible when opened or hovered
    BoxGlow.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice
    BoxGlow.SliceCenter = Rect.new(21, 21, 79, 79)
    BoxGlow.ZIndex = 1
    BoxGlow.Parent = BoxFrame
    RegisterBrand(BoxGlow, "ImageColor3", "brand")

    -- Chữ đang chọn căn giữa, chừa khoảng cho icon
    local SelText = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    SelText.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, 0)
    SelText.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    SelText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    SelText.Text = selected
    SelText.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
    SelText.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    SelText.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.8
    SelText.FontFace = CustomFontFace
    SelText.TextSize = math.floor(11.5 * TextScale)
    SelText.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    SelText.Parent = BoxFrame

    -- Icon Chevron Down cho Dropdown
    local Arrow = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    Arrow.Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16)
    Arrow.Position = UDim2.new(1, -22, 0.5, -8)
    Arrow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    Arrow.Image = "rbxassetid://72690112230014"
    Arrow.ImageColor3 = Theme.Text
    Arrow.Parent = BoxFrame

    local SelBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    SelBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    SelBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    SelBtn.Text = ""
    SelBtn.ZIndex = 5
    SelBtn.Parent = BoxFrame

    local isOpen = false

    SelBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        if not isOpen then
            TweenService:Create(BoxFrame, fastSmooth, {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.ElementHover}):Play()
            TweenService:Create(SelText, fastSmooth, {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)}):Play()
        end
    end)
    SelBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        if not isOpen then
            TweenService:Create(BoxFrame, fastSmooth, {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Element}):Play()
            TweenService:Create(SelText, fastSmooth, {TextColor3 = Theme.Text}):Play()
        end
    end)

    SelBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if isOpen then
            isOpen = false
            TweenService:Create(Arrow, fastSmooth, {Rotation = 0}):Play()
            TweenService:Create(BoxFrame, fastSmooth, {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Element}):Play()
            
            local existingMenu = OverlayGui:FindFirstChild("FloatingMenu")
            if existingMenu then
                local eScale = existingMenu:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale")
                if eScale then
                    TweenService:Create(eScale, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {Scale = 0}):Play()
                end
                task.delay(0.3, function()
                    -- Chỉ xóa nếu vẫn đang đóng
                    if not isOpen and OverlayGui then OverlayGui:ClearAllChildren() end
                end)
            else
                OverlayGui:ClearAllChildren()
            end
            return
        end

        OverlayGui:ClearAllChildren()
        isOpen = true
        TweenService:Create(Arrow, fastSmooth, {Rotation = 180}):Play()

        local CloseBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        CloseBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        CloseBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.999 -- Không để 1 để tránh bị exploit bỏ qua hit-detection
        CloseBtn.Text = ""
        CloseBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        CloseBtn.ZIndex = 105
        CloseBtn.Parent = OverlayGui

        -- Premium Context Menu: Rộng hơn Box, căn mép phải
        local menuWidth = 130
        local absX = (BoxFrame.AbsolutePosition.X + BoxFrame.AbsoluteSize.X) - menuWidth
        local absY = BoxFrame.AbsolutePosition.Y + BoxFrame.AbsoluteSize.Y + 4

        local FloatingMenu = Instance.new("TextButton") -- Dùng TextButton để block click tuyệt đối
        FloatingMenu.Name = "FloatingMenu"
        FloatingMenu.Text = ""
        FloatingMenu.AutoButtonColor = false
        local menuHeight = math.min(#options * 28 + 8, 160)
        FloatingMenu.ClipsDescendants = true
        FloatingMenu.Active = true
        FloatingMenu.Size = UDim2.new(0, menuWidth, 0, menuHeight)
        FloatingMenu.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
        FloatingMenu.Position = UDim2.new(0, absX + menuWidth / 2, 0, absY)
        
        local FloatingScale = Instance.new("UIScale")
        FloatingScale.Scale = 0
        FloatingScale.Parent = FloatingMenu
        FloatingMenu.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 29)
        FloatingMenu.BorderSizePixel = 0
        FloatingMenu.ZIndex = 110
        FloatingMenu.Parent = OverlayGui

        local FloatingCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        FloatingCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
        FloatingCorner.Parent = FloatingMenu

        -- Viền xám tinh tế (không dùng viền xanh BrandDim chóe mắt)
        local FloatingStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        FloatingStroke.Thickness = 1
        FloatingStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 50)
        FloatingStroke.Parent = FloatingMenu

        local Scroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
        Scroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -8)
        Scroll.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 4)
        Scroll.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        Scroll.Active = true
        Scroll.ScrollBarThickness = 2
        Scroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, #options * 28)
        Scroll.ZIndex = 111
        Scroll.Parent = FloatingMenu

        local Layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        Layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        Layout.Parent = Scroll

        local function closeMenu()
            isOpen = false
            TweenService:Create(Arrow, fastSmooth, {Rotation = 0}):Play()
            if FloatingMenu.Parent then
                TweenService:Create(FloatingScale, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {Scale = 0}):Play()
                TweenService:Create(BoxFrame, fastSmooth, {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Element}):Play()
                task.delay(0.3, function()
                    if OverlayGui then OverlayGui:ClearAllChildren() end
                end)
            end
        end

        for i, opt in ipairs(options) do
            local OptBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
            OptBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 28)
            OptBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 0)
            OptBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            OptBtn.Text = opt
            OptBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.TextMuted
            OptBtn.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            OptBtn.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.8
OptBtn.FontFace = CustomFontFace
            OptBtn.TextSize = math.floor(12 * TextScale)
            OptBtn.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            OptBtn.ZIndex = 112
            OptBtn.Parent = Scroll

            local UIPadding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
            UIPadding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 12)
            UIPadding.Parent = OptBtn

            local OptCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
            OptCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
            OptCorner.Parent = OptBtn

            -- Hiệu ứng hover highlight tinh tế
            OptBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                TweenService:Create(OptBtn, fastSmooth, {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 45), BackgroundTransparency = 0}):Play()
            end)
            OptBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                TweenService:Create(OptBtn, fastSmooth, {TextColor3 = Theme.TextMuted, BackgroundTransparency = 1}):Play()
            end)

            OptBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                selected = opt
                SelText.Text = selected
                if Notify then Notify("Selected", title .. " -> " .. selected, 2) end
                if callback then callback(selected) end
                -- Delay 1 tick siêu nhỏ để chặn click xuyên qua UI bên dưới
                task.defer(closeMenu)
            end)
        end

        TweenService:Create(FloatingScale, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Scale = 1}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(BoxFrame, fastSmooth, {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(42, 42, 48)}):Play()

        CloseBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(closeMenu)
    end)
    return Row
end

-- DỮ LIỆU ĐIỀN — dùng đăng ký, nội dung tự build khi click

-- Bảng lưu nội dung builder cho từng sub-tab
ContentArea = Instance.new("Frame")
ContentArea.Size = UDim2.new(1, -CurrentSize.Sidebar, 1, -114)
ContentArea.Position = UDim2.new(0, CurrentSize.Sidebar, 0, 64)
ContentArea.BackgroundTransparency = 1
ContentArea.BorderSizePixel = 0
ContentArea.ClipsDescendants = true
ContentArea.Parent = MainWindow

-- TỰ CẬP NHẬT KHI THAY ĐỔI KÍCH THƯỚC MÀN HÌNH
Camera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(function()
    local newSize = GetAdaptiveSize()
    if newSize.W ~= CurrentSize.W then
        CurrentSize = newSize
        TweenService:Create(MainWindow, fastSmooth, {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, CurrentSize.W, 0, CurrentSize.H),
            Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
        }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(SidebarPanel, fastSmooth, {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, CurrentSize.Sidebar, 1, 0)
        }):Play()
    end
end)

       local WindowAPI = {}
    
    function WindowAPI:SetBackgroundImage(url)
        local Custom_Asset = getcustomasset or getsynasset
        local Request_Func = request or syn and syn.request or http and http.request or http_request
        local Background_Folder = 'Altheris/Backgrounds'

        if not Custom_Asset or not Request_Func then return end

        local function UpdateTransparency(hasImage)
            for _, obj in ipairs(ScreenGui:GetDescendants()) do
                if obj:IsA("TextLabel") or obj:IsA("TextButton") or obj:IsA("TextBox") then
                    if not obj:GetAttribute("OrigColor") then
                        obj:SetAttribute("OrigColor", obj.TextColor3)
                    end
                    local shouldBeBlack = false
                    if hasImage then
                        local ancestor = obj.Parent
                        while ancestor and ancestor ~= ScreenGui do
                            if ancestor.Name == "SectionFrame" or ancestor.Name == "SettingsPanel" or ancestor.Name == "SearchBox" then
                                shouldBeBlack = true
                                break
                            end
                            ancestor = ancestor.Parent
                        end
                    end
                    obj.TextColor3 = shouldBeBlack and Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0) or obj:GetAttribute("OrigColor")
                end

                if obj:IsA("Frame") or obj:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
                    if obj.Name == "SectionFrame" or obj.Name == "SettingsPanel" or obj.Name == "SearchBox" then
                        if not obj:GetAttribute("OrigBgColor") then
                            obj:SetAttribute("OrigBgColor", obj.BackgroundColor3)
                        end
                        obj.BackgroundTransparency = hasImage and 0.45 or 0
                        obj.BackgroundColor3 = hasImage and Color3.fromRGB(230, 230, 235) or obj:GetAttribute("OrigBgColor")
                    elseif obj.Name == "SidebarPanel" then
                        obj.BackgroundTransparency = hasImage and 1 or 0.25
                    elseif obj.Name == "Dropdown_BoxFrame" then
                        obj.BackgroundTransparency = hasImage and 1 or 0
                    elseif obj.Name == "ContentHeader" or obj.Name == "BottomBar" or obj.Name == "ToggleUIBar" or obj.Name == "ProfileCard" or obj.Name == "SidebarCheater" or obj.Name == "VerticalDivider" then
                        obj.BackgroundTransparency = hasImage and 1 or 0
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        if url == "" or url == "None" then
            BackgroundImage.ImageTransparency = 1
            UpdateTransparency(false)
            return
        end

        if not isfolder(Background_Folder) then
            makefolder(Background_Folder)
        end
        
        local extension = url:match('%.([a-zA-Z0-9]+)$') or 'jpg'
        local safeName = url:gsub('%W', ''):sub(-48)
        local path = Background_Folder..'/'..safeName..'.'..extension

        task.spawn(function()
            if isfile(path) then
                BackgroundImage.Image = Custom_Asset(path)
                BackgroundImage.ImageTransparency = 0.15
                UpdateTransparency(true)
                return
            end
            local success, response = pcall(function()
                return Request_Func({ Url = url, Method = 'GET' })
            end)
            if success and response.StatusCode == 200 then
                writefile(path, response.Body)
                BackgroundImage.Image = Custom_Asset(path)
                BackgroundImage.ImageTransparency = 0.15
                UpdateTransparency(true)
            end
        end)
    end
    
    function WindowAPI:SetBackgroundScaleType(mode)
        if mode == "Crop" then
            BackgroundImage.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
        elseif mode == "Fit" then
            BackgroundImage.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
        elseif mode == "Stretch" then
            BackgroundImage.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Stretch
        end
    end
    
    function WindowAPI:CreateTabGroup(groupName, iconId)
        local MainTab = CreateMainTab(groupName, iconId)
        local GroupAPI = {}
        
        function GroupAPI:CreateTab(tabName, description, iconId)
            local SubTab = MainTab.AddSubTab(tabName, description, iconId)
            local TabAPI = {}
            
            function TabAPI:CreateSection(sectionName, side)
                local SectionObj = CreateSection(SubTab, sectionName, side or "Left", 1)
                local SectionAPI = {}
                
                local order = 0
                local function nextOrder() order = order + 1 return order end
                
                function SectionAPI:CreateLabel(text)
                    return CreateLabel(SectionObj, text, nextOrder())
                end
                
                function SectionAPI:CreateButton(name, callback)
                    CreateButton(SectionObj, name, callback, nextOrder())
                end
                function SectionAPI:CreateToggle(name, default, callback)
                    local api = CreateToggle(SectionObj, name, default, callback, nextOrder())
                    if WindowAPI and WindowAPI.Elements then WindowAPI.Elements[name] = api end
                    return api
                end
                
                function SectionAPI:CreateSlider(name, min, max, default, callback)
                    local api = CreateSlider(SectionObj, name, min, max, default, callback, nextOrder())
                    if WindowAPI and WindowAPI.Elements then WindowAPI.Elements[name] = api end
                    return api
                end
                
                function SectionAPI:CreateDropdown(name, options, default, callback)
                    local api = CreateDropdown(SectionObj, name, options, default, callback, nextOrder())
                    if WindowAPI and WindowAPI.Elements then WindowAPI.Elements[name] = api end
                    return api
                end
                
                -- Đã thêm TextBox vào đây
                function SectionAPI:CreateTextBox(name, placeholder, clearOnFocus, callback)
                    local api = CreateTextBox(SectionObj, name, placeholder, clearOnFocus, callback, nextOrder())
                    if WindowAPI and WindowAPI.Elements then WindowAPI.Elements[name] = api end
                    return api
                end

                
                return SectionAPI
            end
            
            return TabAPI
        end
        
        return GroupAPI
    end

    
    -- DRAG UI LOGIC
    local dragging
    local dragInput
    local dragStart
    local startPos

    local function update(input)
        local delta = input.Position - dragStart
        MainWindow.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
    end

    local function applyDrag(frame)
        frame.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = true
                dragStart = input.Position
                startPos = MainWindow.Position

                input.Changed:Connect(function()
                    if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                        dragging = false
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)

        frame.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragInput = input
            end
        end)
    end

    applyDrag(ContentHeader)
    applyDrag(SidebarPanel)

    UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if input == dragInput and dragging then
            update(input)
        end
    end)

    WindowAPI.Notify = Notify
    
    return WindowAPI
end

pcall(function() setfflag("TaskSchedulerTargetFps", "5099990") end)

local UserInputService = cloneref(game:GetService('UserInputService'))
local RunService = cloneref(game:GetService('RunService'))
local Stats = cloneref(game:GetService('Stats'))

local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
local Mouse = LocalPlayer:GetMouse()


local islclosure = islclosure or function(f) return type(f) == "function" end
local setupvalue = setupvalue or debug.setupvalue

if not debug.getupvalues then
    debug.getupvalues = function(f)
        local t, i = {}, 1
        while true do
            local n, v = debug.getupvalue(f, i)
            if not n then break end
            t[i] = v
            i = i + 1
        end
        return t
    end
end

local function safeGetConnections(event)
    if not getconnections then return {} end
    local ok, r = pcall(getconnections, event)
    return (ok and r) or {}
end

local function disableConn(conn)
    if conn.Disable then 
        pcall(function() conn:Disable() end)
    elseif conn.Disconnect then 
        pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end) 
    end
end

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

local Alive = workspace:FindFirstChild("Alive") or workspace:WaitForChild("Alive")
local Runtime = workspace:FindFirstChild("Runtime") or workspace:WaitForChild("Runtime")

local swordInstancesInstance = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared", 9e9):WaitForChild("ReplicatedInstances", 9e9):WaitForChild("Swords", 9e9)

if setthreadidentity then pcall(setthreadidentity, 2) end

local success, swordInstances = pcall(require, swordInstancesInstance)

if setthreadidentity then pcall(setthreadidentity, oldIdentity) end

if not success then return end

local getSlashName = function(swordName)
    if type(swordName) ~= "string" or swordName == "" then return "SlashEffect" end
    local ok, result = pcall(function() return swordInstances:GetSword(swordName) end)
    if ok and type(result) == "table" then
        return result.SlashName or result.SlashEffect or "SlashEffect"
    end
    return "SlashEffect"
end

local skinchangerSystem = {
    equipHooked = false,
    originalEquipSwordTo = nil,
    fxHooked = false,
    playParryFunc = nil,
    swordsController = nil,
    myParryHandler = nil,
    slashName = nil,
    changeSwordModel = false,
    changeSwordAnimation = false,
    changeSwordFX = false,
    fxConnection = nil,
    swordModel = nil,
    swordAnimations = nil,
    swordFX = nil,
    setSword = nil,
    skinChangerEnabled = false
}

local setSword = function()
    if not skinchangerSystem.skinChangerEnabled then return end
    if not LocalPlayer.Character then return end
    
    pcall(function()
        local f = rawget(swordInstances, "EquipSwordTo")
        if type(f) == "function" then
            local ups = debug.getupvalues(f)
            for i = 1, #ups do
                if type(ups[i]) == "boolean" then
                    setupvalue(f, i, false)
                    break
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    
    pcall(function()
        swordInstances:EquipSwordTo(LocalPlayer.Character, skinchangerSystem.swordModel)
    end)
    
    task.spawn(function()
        local attempts = 0
        while not skinchangerSystem.swordsController and attempts < 20 do
            task.wait(0.5)
            attempts = attempts + 1
        end
        if not skinchangerSystem.swordsController then return end
        
        pcall(function()
            if skinchangerSystem.swordsController.SetSword then
                local animTarget = (skinchangerSystem.swordAnimations and skinchangerSystem.swordAnimations ~= "") and skinchangerSystem.swordAnimations or skinchangerSystem.swordModel
                skinchangerSystem.swordsController:SetSword(animTarget)
            end
        end)
        
        pcall(function()
            local targetSword = (skinchangerSystem.swordFX and skinchangerSystem.swordFX ~= "") and skinchangerSystem.swordFX or skinchangerSystem.swordModel
            if ReplicatedStorage.Remotes:FindFirstChild("FireSwordInfo") then
                ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.FireSwordInfo:FireServer(targetSword)
            end
            if skinchangerSystem.swordsController.currentSword ~= nil then
                pcall(function() skinchangerSystem.swordsController.currentSword = targetSword end)
            end
            if skinchangerSystem.swordsController.SwordFX ~= nil then
                pcall(function() skinchangerSystem.swordsController.SwordFX = targetSword end)
            end
        end)
    end)
end

skinchangerSystem.updateSword = function()
    local fxName = (skinchangerSystem.swordFX and skinchangerSystem.swordFX ~= "") and skinchangerSystem.swordFX or skinchangerSystem.swordModel
    if fxName and fxName ~= "" then
        skinchangerSystem.slashName = getSlashName(fxName)
    else
        skinchangerSystem.slashName = "SlashEffect"
    end
    setSword()
end

task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(0.25) and not skinchangerSystem.swordsController do
        local ok, conns = pcall(getconnections, ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.FireSwordInfo.OnClientEvent)
        if ok and conns then
            for _, v in ipairs(conns) do
                if v.Function and islclosure(v.Function) then
                    local ok2, up = pcall(debug.getupvalues, v.Function)
                    if ok2 and type(up) == "table" and #up == 1 and type(up[1]) == "table" then
                        skinchangerSystem.swordsController = up[1]
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)

local hookedFuncs = {}
task.spawn(function()
    local remotesToHook = {"ParrySuccessAll", "ParryAttempt", "ParrySuccess", "PlaySound", "PlayVisuals"}
    while task.wait(1) do
        for _, remoteName in ipairs(remotesToHook) do
            local remote = ReplicatedStorage.Remotes:FindFirstChild(remoteName)
            if remote and remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                local ok, conns = pcall(getconnections, remote.OnClientEvent)
                if ok and type(conns) == "table" then
                    for _, v in ipairs(conns) do
                        local func = v.Function
                        if func and not hookedFuncs[func] then
                            hookedFuncs[func] = true
                            v:Disable()
                            
                            local targetFunc = func
                            local ourFunc = function(...)
                                local args = { ... }
                                local isLocal = false
                                
                                for _, arg in ipairs(args) do
                                    if tostring(arg) == LocalPlayer.Name or (typeof(arg) == "Instance" and (arg == LocalPlayer.Character or arg == LocalPlayer)) then
                                        isLocal = true
                                        break
                                    end
                                end

                                if isLocal and skinchangerSystem.skinChangerEnabled then
                                    local fxSword = (skinchangerSystem.swordFX and skinchangerSystem.swordFX ~= "") and skinchangerSystem.swordFX or skinchangerSystem.swordModel
                                    local swordFound = false
                                    local slashFound = false

                                    for i, arg in ipairs(args) do
                                        if type(arg) == "string" then
                                            if fxSword and fxSword ~= "" and not slashFound and (arg:match("Slash") or arg == "Default" or arg:match("Effect")) then
                                                args[i] = skinchangerSystem.slashName or "SlashEffect"
                                                slashFound = true
                                            elseif fxSword and fxSword ~= "" and not swordFound then
                                                local isSword = false
                                                pcall(function()
                                                    if ReplicatedStorage.Shared.ReplicatedInstances.Swords:FindFirstChild(arg) then
                                                        isSword = true
                                                    end
                                                end)
                                                if isSword or arg == LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("CurrentlyEquippedSword") then
                                                    args[i] = fxSword
                                                    swordFound = true
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end

                                    if fxSword and fxSword ~= "" and not slashFound and type(args[1]) == "string" then
                                        args[1] = skinchangerSystem.slashName or "SlashEffect"
                                    end
                                    if fxSword and fxSword ~= "" and not swordFound and type(args[3]) == "string" then
                                        args[3] = fxSword
                                    end
                                end
                                
                                if setthreadidentity then pcall(setthreadidentity, 2) end
                                pcall(targetFunc, unpack(args))
                            end
                            
                            hookedFuncs[ourFunc] = true
                            remote.OnClientEvent:Connect(ourFunc)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)

task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(1) do
        if skinchangerSystem.skinChangerEnabled and skinchangerSystem.swordModel and skinchangerSystem.swordModel ~= "" then
            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            if char then
                if LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("CurrentlyEquippedSword") ~= skinchangerSystem.swordModel then
                    setSword()
                end
                if not char:FindFirstChild(skinchangerSystem.swordModel) then
                    setSword()
                end
                for _, v in pairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                    if v:IsA("Model") and v.Name ~= skinchangerSystem.swordModel then
                        v:Destroy()
                    end
                    task.wait()
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)

LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    if skinchangerSystem.skinChangerEnabled then
        local wasEnabled = skinchangerSystem.skinChangerEnabled
        skinchangerSystem.skinChangerEnabled = false
        task.wait(2)
        skinchangerSystem.skinChangerEnabled = wasEnabled
        task.wait(0.5)
        pcall(function() skinchangerSystem.updateSword() end)
    end
end)

task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(0.5) do
        if not skinchangerSystem.skinChangerEnabled then continue end
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        
        if char and skinchangerSystem.swordModel then
            if not char:FindFirstChild(skinchangerSystem.swordModel) then
                pcall(setSword)
            end
            
            for _, child in pairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                if child:IsA("Model") and child.Name ~= skinchangerSystem.swordModel then
                    local success, isSword = pcall(function() 
                        return swordInstances:GetSword(child.Name) 
                    end)
                    if success and isSword then
                        child:Destroy()
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)
