getgenv().ParrySoundConfig = {
    Enabled = false,
    Selected = "Neverlose",
    Volume = 5,
    Pitch = 100,
    CustomId = ""
}

local function PlayParrySound()
    task.spawn(function()
        local cfg = getgenv().ParrySoundConfig
        if not cfg or not cfg.Enabled then return end
        
        local id = (cfg.Selected == "Custom" and cfg.CustomId) or ParrySounds[cfg.Selected] or cfg.CustomId
        if not id or tostring(id) == "" then return end
        
        local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
        local rawId = tostring(id):gsub("%s+", "")
        if not string.find(rawId, "rbxassetid://") and not string.find(rawId, "http") then
            sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://" .. rawId
        else
            sound.SoundId = rawId
        end
        
        sound.Volume = math.clamp((cfg.Volume or 5) / 5, 0.1, 5)
        sound.PlaybackSpeed = math.clamp((cfg.Pitch or 100) / 100, 0.2, 3)
        sound.Parent = SoundService
        sound:Play()
        
        Debris:AddItem(sound, 3)
    end)
end



ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.ParrySuccess.OnClientEvent:Connect(function()
    PlayParrySound()
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not character or not Alive or character.Parent ~= Alive then return end

    local humanoid = character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
    local animator = humanoid and humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
    if not animator then return end
    
    for _, track in ipairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
        local name = track.Name
        if name == "GrabParry" or name == "Grab" then
            track:Stop()
        end
    end
end)

local RunService = cloneref and cloneref(game:GetService("RunService")) or game:GetService("RunService")
local Stats      = cloneref and cloneref(game:GetService("Stats")) or game:GetService("Stats")
local Players    = game:GetService("Players")

local function execute_spam_packet()
    local mode = (getgenv and getgenv().AutoSpamMode) or "Remote"
    local packets = (getgenv and getgenv().ManualPackets) or (System.config and System.config.manual_packets) or 7

    -- [PHYSICS UPDATE BYPASS]: Ép Server cập nhật vị trí Character liên tục để bypass độ trễ tick mới
    pcall(function()
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hrp = char and (char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char.PrimaryPart)
        if hrp then
            hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 0.01, 0)
        end
    end)

    for i = 1, math.clamp(packets, 1, 10) do
        if mode == "Keypress" then
            pcall(function()
                if VirtualInputManager then
                    VirtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 0)
                    VirtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 0)
                end
            end)
        else
            if type(getgenv().Parry) == "function" then
                getgenv().Parry()
            else
                local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
                if remotes then
                    local parryRemote = remotes:FindFirstChild("ParryButtonPress") or remotes:FindFirstChild("ParryAttempt")
                    if parryRemote then parryRemote:FireServer() end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

System.manual_spam = {}

local msthreads = {}
local msconns   = {}

System.manual_spam.loop_stop = function()
    System.__properties.__manual_spam_enabled = false

    for _, t in ipairs(msthreads) do
        pcall(function() task.cancel(t) end)
    end
    msthreads = {}

    for _, c in ipairs(msconns) do
        pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
    end
    msconns = {}
end

System.manual_spam.loop_start = function()
    System.manual_spam.loop_stop()

    System.__properties.__manual_spam_enabled = true

    local anim_fn = System.animation and System.animation.play_grab_parry
    local ms_last_fire_time = 0
    local ms_next_micro_delay = 0

    local function burst_fire()
        local now = os.clock()
        if now - ms_last_fire_time < ms_next_micro_delay then
            return
        end

        local rps = getgenv().SpammingRPS or 480
        local delay_time = 1 / rps
        ms_next_micro_delay = delay_time * math.random(8, 12) / 10

        ms_last_fire_time = now

        execute_spam_packet()
        if anim_fn then task.defer(anim_fn) end
    end

    -- Zenthra's Dual-Simulation Physics Runner + Our Burst Fire Upgrade
    table.insert(msthreads, task.spawn(function()
        local PreSim = RunService.PreSimulation
        local PostSim = RunService.PostSimulation

        while System.__properties.__manual_spam_enabled do
            burst_fire()
            PostSim:Wait()

            if not System.__properties.__manual_spam_enabled then break end

            burst_fire()
            PreSim:Wait()
        end
    end))

end

System.manual_spam.start = function()
    System.manual_spam.loop_start()
end

System.manual_spam.stop = function()
    System.manual_spam.loop_stop()
end


System.__properties = System.__properties or {}
System.__properties.__manual_spam_enabled = System.__properties.__manual_spam_enabled or false

System.__properties = System.__properties or {}
System.__properties.__auto_spam_enabled = System.__properties.__auto_spam_enabled or false

-- ==============================================================
-- ZENTHRA 40+ CPS DUAL-SIMULATION SPAM ENGINE
-- ==============================================================
System.spam = {
    parries = 0,
    last_parried_ball = nil,
    clash_ball = nil,
    clash_ball_left = false,
    auto_decay_at = {}
}

System.spam.register_parry = function(ball)
    System.spam.parries = (System.spam.parries or 0) + 1
    System.spam.last_parried_ball = ball
    table.insert(System.spam.auto_decay_at, os.clock() + 0.35)
end

System.auto_spam = {}

function System.spam.manual_step(dt)
    if not System.__properties.__manual_spam_enabled then return end
    
    -- [MESSAGE 2 SPAM TECHNIQUE]: Time Accumulator RPS Logic (480 RPS)
    local rps = getgenv().SpammingRPS or 480
    System.spam.manual_acc = (System.spam.manual_acc or 0) + (dt or 0.016)
    local fire_count = math.floor(System.spam.manual_acc * rps)
    
    if fire_count > 0 then
        System.spam.manual_acc = System.spam.manual_acc - (fire_count / rps)
        for _ = 1, fire_count do
            execute_spam_packet()
        end
    end
end

local as_threads = {}

function System.spam.auto_step(dt)
    if not System.__properties.__auto_spam_enabled then return end
    if not System.spam.parries then System.spam.parries = 0 end

    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    local root = char and (char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char.PrimaryPart)
    if not root then return end

    local ball = System.ball and System.ball.get and System.ball.get()
    if not ball or not ball.Parent then return end

    local zoomies = ball:FindFirstChild("zoomies")
    local vel = (zoomies and zoomies.VectorVelocity) or ball.AssemblyLinearVelocity or Vector3.zero
    local speed = math.max(vel.Magnitude, 1)

    local root_pos = root.Position
    local ball_pos = ball.Position
    local dist = (root_pos - ball_pos).Magnitude

    local target = ball:GetAttribute("target")
    local my_name = LocalPlayer.Name

    -- Outside standoff range entirely
    if dist > 35 and target ~= my_name then
        System.__properties.__is_spamming = false
        System.spam.auto_acc = 0 -- Xóa accumulator khi hết spam
        return
    end

    local ping = (smoothedPing or 50)
    local ping_factor = math.clamp(ping / 10, 1, 16)
    local a_factor = 2.4 + math.min(math.max(speed - 9.5, 0), 650) * 0.002
    local min_dist = ping_factor + math.max(speed / a_factor, 9.5)

    local simple_tti = dist / speed
    local threshold = System.__properties.__spam_threshold or 3
    local ball_combo = (System.__properties.__ball_parry_count and System.__properties.__ball_parry_count[ball]) or (System.spam and System.spam.parries) or 0
    local is_targeted = (target == my_name) or (ball == System.spam.last_parried_ball)

    -- BẮT BUỘC ĐỦ HẾT ĐIỀU KIỆN (AND STRICT 100%)
    local should_spam = is_targeted
        and (simple_tti <= 0.225)
        and (dist <= min_dist)
        and (ball_combo >= threshold)

    if should_spam then
        local now = os.clock()
        System.spam.parries = System.spam.parries + 1
        System.spam.last_parried_ball = ball
        table.insert(System.spam.auto_decay_at, now + 0.2)
        System.__properties.__is_spamming = true

        local anim_fn = System.animation and System.animation.play_grab_parry

        -- [MESSAGE 2 SPAM TECHNIQUE]: Time Accumulator RPS Logic (480 RPS)
        local rps = getgenv().SpammingRPS or 480
        System.spam.auto_acc = (System.spam.auto_acc or 0) + (dt or 0.016)
        local fire_count = math.floor(System.spam.auto_acc * rps)
        
        if fire_count > 0 then
            System.spam.auto_acc = System.spam.auto_acc - (fire_count / rps)
            for _ = 1, fire_count do
                execute_spam_packet()
            end
            if anim_fn then task.defer(anim_fn) end
        end
    else
        System.__properties.__is_spamming = false
        System.spam.auto_acc = 0 -- Reset bộ đếm
    end
end

System.auto_spam.stop = function()
    System.__properties.__auto_spam_enabled = false
    System.__properties.__is_spamming = false
    for _, t in ipairs(as_threads) do
        pcall(function() task.cancel(t) end)
    end
    as_threads = {}
end

System.auto_spam.start = function()
    System.auto_spam.stop()
    System.__properties.__auto_spam_enabled = true

    if setfpscap then setfpscap(240) end

    -- Zenthra Dual-Simulation 40+ CPS Physics Runner
    table.insert(as_threads, task.spawn(function()
        local PreSim = RunService.PreSimulation
        local PostSim = RunService.PostSimulation

        while System.__properties.__auto_spam_enabled do
            local now = os.clock()
            for i = #System.spam.auto_decay_at, 1, -1 do
                if now >= System.spam.auto_decay_at[i] then
                    if System.spam.parries > 0 then
                        System.spam.parries = System.spam.parries - 1
                    end
                    table.remove(System.spam.auto_decay_at, i)
                end
            end

            System.spam.auto_step()
            PostSim:Wait()

            if not System.__properties.__auto_spam_enabled then break end

            System.spam.auto_step()
            PreSim:Wait()
        end
    end))
end
