local function ClearTriggerbotState(ball)
    triggerbot_retry_state[ball] = nil
end

local smoothedPing     = 50
local ping_jitter      = 0
local last_ping_update = 0
local prev_ping        = 50

local update_ping_data = function(now_clock)
    if now_clock - last_ping_update < 0.15 then
        return
    end

    last_ping_update = now_clock

    local ok, raw = pcall(function()
        return game:GetService("Stats").Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue()
    end)

    local current_ping = ok and raw or 50
    ping_jitter = math_abs(current_ping - prev_ping)
    prev_ping = current_ping
    smoothedPing = smoothedPing + (current_ping - smoothedPing) * 0.3
end

local function GetTrajectoryProjection(ballPos, relativeVel, charPos)
    local relativePos = charPos - ballPos
    local velUnit = relativeVel.Unit
    local distanceAlongTrajectory = relativePos:Dot(velUnit)

    if distanceAlongTrajectory < 0 then
        return relativePos.Magnitude, -1
    end

    local projectedPoint = ballPos + (velUnit * distanceAlongTrajectory)
    local closestApproach = (charPos - projectedPoint).Magnitude

    return closestApproach, distanceAlongTrajectory
end

-- ==============================================================
-- MODULARIZED HIGH-SPEED PRIVATE AUTO PARRY OPTIMIZER
-- ==============================================================
local function is_player_inhibited(character, root_part)
    if not character or not root_part then return true end
    local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if humanoid and humanoid.Health <= 0 then return true end
    -- Singularity cape (hard block)
    if root_part:FindFirstChild("SingularityCape") then return true end
    -- Phantom mode (Zenthra)
    if System.__config.__detections.__phantom and System.__properties.__phantom_active then return true end
    -- Pulsed attribute on ball target (skip parry when pulsed)
    if System.__config.__detections.__pulsed and System.__properties.__pulsed_active then return true end
    -- Forcefield ability active
    if System.__config.__detections.__forcefield and System.__properties.__forcefield_active then return true end
    -- Infinity
    if System.__config.__detections.__infinity and System.__properties.__infinity_active then return true end
    -- Death Slash
    if System.__config.__detections.__deathslash and System.__properties.__deathslash_active then return true end
    -- Time Hole
    if System.__config.__detections.__timehole and System.__properties.__timehole_active then return true end
    -- Slashes of Fury
    if System.__config.__detections.__slashesoffury and System.__properties.__slashesoffury_active then return true end
    return false
end

-- Zenthra: Cập nhật forcefield state mỗi frame
local function update_forcefield_state()
    local char = LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then
        System.__properties.__forcefield_active = false
        return
    end
    local equippedAbility = LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("CurrentlyEquippedAbility") or char:GetAttribute("CurrentlyEquippedAbility")
    local FORCEFIELD_ABILITIES = { Forcefield = true, ["Force Field"] = true }
    System.__properties.__forcefield_active = (FORCEFIELD_ABILITIES[equippedAbility or ""] ~= nil)
        and (char:GetAttribute("AbilityActive") == true)
end

-- Zenthra: Kiểm tra ball có pulsed / combocounter / dribble không (gọi trong evaluate_ball_threat)
local function check_ball_special_attributes(ball_part)
    if System.__config.__detections.__pulsed and ball_part:GetAttribute("Pulsed") then
        return true
    end
    if System.__config.__detections.__combocounter and ball_part:FindFirstChild("ComboCounter") then
        return true
    end
    if System.__config.__detections.__dribble and ball_part:GetAttribute("DribbleActive") == true then
        return true
    end
    return false
end

-- Zenthra: Kiểm tra aerodynamic slash + tornado window
local function check_aerodynamic_and_tornado(ball_part, now_clock)
    if not System.__config.__detections.__aerodynamic then return false end
    -- Detect AeroDynamicSlashVFX trên ball, lưu thời điểm
    if ball_part:FindFirstChild("AeroDynamicSlashVFX") then
        -- Xoá VFX khỏi ball để không bị detect liên tục
        pcall(function() ball_part.AeroDynamicSlashVFX:Destroy() end)
        System.__properties.__aerodynamic_time = now_clock
    end
    -- Tìm Tornado runtime ref
    local runtime = workspace:FindFirstChild("Runtime")
    local tornado = runtime and runtime:FindFirstChild("Tornado")
    if tornado then
        local tornadoTime = tornado:GetAttribute("TornadoTime") or 1
        if now_clock - (System.__properties.__aerodynamic_time or 0) < tornadoTime + 0.314159 then
            return true -- còn trong cửa sổ tornado, bỏ qua parry
        end
    end
    return false
end

-- ==============================================================
-- ZENTHRA DRIBBLE STATE MACHINE (4 PHASES ENGINE)
-- ==============================================================
System.dribble_detection = {
    enabled = true,
    phase = "Idle", -- "Idle", "Pending", "Outbound", "Return"
    tracked_ball = nil,
    fired_at = 0,
    accepted_at = 0,
    origin = nil,
    max_travel = 0,
    outbound_seen = false,
    rearm_ball = nil,
    rearm_outbound_seen = false,
}

function System.dribble_detection.reset()
    local dribble_detection = System.dribble_detection
    dribble_detection.phase = "Idle"
    dribble_detection.tracked_ball = nil
    dribble_detection.fired_at = 0
    dribble_detection.accepted_at = 0
    dribble_detection.origin = nil
    dribble_detection.max_travel = 0
    dribble_detection.outbound_seen = false
    dribble_detection.rearm_ball = nil
    dribble_detection.rearm_outbound_seen = false
end

function System.dribble_detection.owns(ball)
    return System.dribble_detection.tracked_ball == ball
end

function System.dribble_detection.activation_timeout(ping_s)
    return math.clamp(ping_s + 0.35, 0.20, 0.80)
end

function System.dribble_detection.safe_tti(ping_s)
    return math.clamp(ping_s * 1.2 + 0.15, 0.18, 0.55)
end

function System.dribble_detection.cycle_tti(ping_s, speed)
    return math.clamp(0.25 + ping_s * 0.5, 0.20, 0.65)
end

function System.dribble_detection.cycle_distance(speed, closing)
    return math.clamp(speed * 0.15 + 10, 10, 35)
end

function System.dribble_detection.remote_gap(ping_s)
    return math.clamp(ping_s * 0.8 + 0.05, 0.05, 0.25)
end

function System.dribble_detection.decide(data)
    local dribbleEngine = System.dribble_detection
    if not data.enabled then
        if dribbleEngine.phase ~= "Idle" then dribbleEngine.reset() end
        return "passthrough"
    end

    local currentClock = data.now or os.clock()
    local pingSeconds = data.ping_s or 0.05
    local targetBall = data.ball
    local closingVelocity = data.closing or 0
    local targetDistance = data.dist or 0
    local activationTimeout = dribbleEngine.activation_timeout(pingSeconds)
    local safeTimeToImpact = dribbleEngine.safe_tti(pingSeconds)
    local isApproachingPlayer = closingVelocity > 10

    if dribbleEngine.phase ~= "Idle" and dribbleEngine.owns(targetBall) and data.lost_target then
        if dribbleEngine.phase ~= "Outbound" or (currentClock - dribbleEngine.accepted_at >= 1.5) then
            dribbleEngine.reset()
            return "passthrough"
        end
    end

    if dribbleEngine.phase == "Pending" then
        if not dribbleEngine.owns(targetBall) then return "passthrough" end
        if data.ability_active or data.ball_dribble_active or ((data.success_at or 0) >= (dribbleEngine.fired_at or 0) and (dribbleEngine.fired_at or 0) > 0) then
            dribbleEngine.phase = "Outbound"
            dribbleEngine.accepted_at = currentClock
            dribbleEngine.origin = dribbleEngine.origin or data.position
            dribbleEngine.max_travel = (dribbleEngine.origin and data.position) and (data.position - dribbleEngine.origin).Magnitude or 0
            if System.detection and System.detection.__per_ball then
                System.detection.__per_ball[targetBall] = nil
            end
            return "hold"
        end
        if currentClock - dribbleEngine.fired_at >= activationTimeout then
            dribbleEngine.reset()
            if isApproachingPlayer and (targetDistance / closingVelocity <= safeTimeToImpact) then
                return "reject_fallback"
            end
            return "passthrough"
        end
        return "hold"
    end

    if dribbleEngine.phase == "Outbound" then
        if not dribbleEngine.owns(targetBall) then return "passthrough" end
        local successAt = data.success_at or 0
        if successAt >= dribbleEngine.fired_at and successAt > dribbleEngine.accepted_at then
            dribbleEngine.accepted_at = successAt
        end
        if dribbleEngine.origin and data.position then
            local travelMagnitude = (data.position - dribbleEngine.origin).Magnitude
            if travelMagnitude > dribbleEngine.max_travel then dribbleEngine.max_travel = travelMagnitude end
        end
        if closingVelocity < -10 and dribbleEngine.max_travel >= 8 then
            dribbleEngine.outbound_seen = true
        end
        if dribbleEngine.outbound_seen and isApproachingPlayer then
            if currentClock - dribbleEngine.accepted_at < dribbleEngine.remote_gap(pingSeconds) then
                return "hold"
            end
            dribbleEngine.phase = "Return"
            if System.detection and System.detection.__per_ball then
                System.detection.__per_ball[targetBall] = nil
            end
            return "parry"
        end
        return "hold"
    end

    if dribbleEngine.phase == "Return" then
        if not dribbleEngine.owns(targetBall) then return "passthrough" end
        if not isApproachingPlayer then return "hold" end
        return "parry"
    end

    if dribbleEngine.rearm_ball then
        if targetBall ~= dribbleEngine.rearm_ball then return "passthrough" end
        if closingVelocity < -10 then
            dribbleEngine.rearm_outbound_seen = true
            return "hold_outbound"
        end
        if not dribbleEngine.rearm_outbound_seen then return "hold" end
        if not isApproachingPlayer then return "hold_outbound" end
        dribbleEngine.rearm_ball = nil
        dribbleEngine.rearm_outbound_seen = false
    end

    if not data.ready then return "passthrough" end
    if not isApproachingPlayer then return "hold_outbound" end
    if (targetDistance / closingVelocity <= dribbleEngine.cycle_tti(pingSeconds, data.speed)) or (targetDistance < dribbleEngine.cycle_distance(data.speed, closingVelocity)) then
        return "parry"
    end

    return "dribble"
end

local function get_divisor_multiplier(accuracy, ping_seconds)
    local clampedAccuracy = math_clamp(accuracy or 100, 1, 100)
    local pingMilliseconds = (ping_seconds or 0.05) * 1000
    
    -- Zenthra Dynamic Ping Compensation
    local pingCompensation = ((pingMilliseconds - 30) / 170) * -0.9
    local accuracyCompensation = (clampedAccuracy - 1) * 0.003535
    
    return 1.15 + pingCompensation + accuracyCompensation
end

local function get_premium_parry_distance(speed, accuracy, ping_seconds)
    local pingMilliseconds = (ping_seconds or 0.05) * 1000
    local pingDistanceFactor = math_clamp(pingMilliseconds / 10, 5, 17)
    
    local clampedSpeed = math_min(math_max(speed - 9.5, 0), 650)
    local baseDivisor = 2.4 + (clampedSpeed * 0.002)
    local finalSpeedDivisor = baseDivisor * get_divisor_multiplier(accuracy, ping_seconds)
    
    local baseParryDistance = pingDistanceFactor + math_max(speed / finalSpeedDivisor, 9.5)
    local rangeMult = math_clamp(tonumber(System.__properties and System.__properties.__parry_range_multiplier) or 1, 0.1, 4)
    
    return baseParryDistance * rangeMult
end

local EvaluateParryDecision = function(threat, is_curving, charPos, ping_seconds, properties, dt, now_clock)
    -- 1. Zenthra Zero-Delay Cooldown Check
    local last_tick = properties.__last_parry_time or 0
    local min_tick_delay = math_clamp(ping_seconds + 0.04, 0.07, 0.16)
    if (now_clock - last_tick) < min_tick_delay then
        return false
    end
    
    if properties.__interaction_locked[threat.ball] then return false end
    if threat.target ~= LocalPlayer.Name then return false end
    if threat.dot <= 0.001 then return false end

    local speed = math_max(threat.relative_speed, 1)

    -- 2. Bộ lọc Zenthra Curve
    if is_curving then return false end
    if speed >= 40 and threat.dot < -0.15 then return false end

    -- 3. Lấy accuracy
    local current_accuracy = properties.__accuracy or 100
    if properties.__randomize_accuracy then
        local min_acc = math_clamp(properties.__random_accuracy_min or 70, 1, 100)
        local max_acc = math_clamp(properties.__random_accuracy_max or 95, 1, 100)
        if min_acc > max_acc then min_acc, max_acc = max_acc, min_acc end
        current_accuracy = math.random(min_acc, max_acc)
    end

    -- 4. Tính khoảng cách Max chuẩn Zenthra
    local max_dist = get_premium_parry_distance(speed, current_accuracy, ping_seconds)
    
    -- Zenthra Dot-scaling: Nếu chĩa thẳng vào mặt, nới lỏng thêm một xíu
    local dot_boost = (threat.dot > 0) and (threat.dot * 3.0) or 0
    max_dist = max_dist + dot_boost

    -- 5. So sánh khoảng cách và Chém
    if threat.distance <= max_dist then
        properties.__last_parry_time = now_clock
        properties.__interaction_locked[threat.ball] = true
        
        -- Zenthra Event Listener Mở Khóa Tức Thì
        local conn
        conn = threat.ball:GetAttributeChangedSignal("target"):Once(function()
            if properties and properties.__interaction_locked and threat and threat.ball then
                properties.__interaction_locked[threat.ball] = false
            end
            if conn then pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end) end
        end)
        
        return true
    end

    return false
end

local function evaluate_ball_threat(Ball, is_training, char_pos, char_vel, local_name, dt)
    if not Ball.Parent then
        System.__properties.__interaction_locked[Ball] = nil
        if System.__properties.__fatal_locks then
            System.__properties.__fatal_locks[Ball] = nil
        end
        if System.__properties.__ball_parry_count then
            System.__properties.__ball_parry_count[Ball] = nil
        end
        if System.__properties.__ball_combo_decay_queue then
            System.__properties.__ball_combo_decay_queue[Ball] = nil
        end
        previous_ball_speeds[Ball] = nil
        previous_ball_distances[Ball] = nil
        return
    end

    -- [ZENTHRA AUTHENTIC: ABILITY LOCKS WITH UI TOGGLES]
    local detections = System.__config.__detections
    if detections.__aerodynamic and Ball:FindFirstChild('AeroDynamicSlashVFX') then
        game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(Ball.AeroDynamicSlashVFX, 0)
        System.__properties.__aerodynamic_time = os.clock()
    end
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if char then
        if detections.__aerodynamic and (os.clock() - (System.__properties.__aerodynamic_time or 0) < (char:GetAttribute('TornadoTime') or 1) + 0.314159) then
            return
        end
        if detections.__infinity and char:GetAttribute('InfinityActive') then return end
        if detections.__deathslash and char:GetAttribute('DeathSlashActive') then return end
        if detections.__slashesoffury and char:GetAttribute('SlashesOfFuryActive') then return end
        if detections.__timehole and char:GetAttribute('TimeHoleActive') then return end
        if detections.__phantom and char:GetAttribute('PhantomActive') then return end
    end
    if detections.__combocounter and Ball:FindFirstChild('ComboCounter') then
        return
    end
    if detections.__pulsed and Ball:GetAttribute('Pulsed') then
        return
    end

    local current_target = Ball:GetAttribute("target")
    if System.__properties.__ball_targets[Ball] ~= current_target then
        System.__properties.__ball_targets[Ball] = current_target
        if current_target == local_name then
            System.__properties.__interaction_locked[Ball] = false
        end
        -- Tăng combo count mỗi lần đổi target (bóng nảy)
        local count = System.__properties.__ball_parry_count[Ball] or 0
        System.__properties.__ball_parry_count[Ball] = count + 1
        
        -- Thêm vào hàng đợi giảm combo sau 0.3s
        System.__properties.__ball_combo_decay_queue[Ball] = System.__properties.__ball_combo_decay_queue[Ball] or {}
        table.insert(System.__properties.__ball_combo_decay_queue[Ball], os.clock() + 0.3)
    end

    if System.__properties.__interaction_locked[Ball] == nil then
        System.__properties.__interaction_locked[Ball] = (current_target ~= local_name)
    end

    if current_target ~= local_name then return end

    -- [DRIBBLE PROTECTION]
    -- Do not Auto Parry while the player is actively executing the Dribble ability!
    if System.__config.__detections.__dribble then
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        if char and char:GetAttribute("AbilityActive") == true then
            local equippedAbility = LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("CurrentlyEquippedAbility") or char:GetAttribute("CurrentlyEquippedAbility")
            if equippedAbility == "Dribble" then
                return
            end
        end
    end

    local Zoomies = Ball:FindFirstChild("zoomies")
    if not Zoomies then return end

    local Velocity = Zoomies.VectorVelocity
    local RelativeVelocity = Velocity - char_vel
    local RelativeSpeed = RelativeVelocity.Magnitude
    local RawSpeed = Velocity.Magnitude

    if RawSpeed <= 0 then return end

    -- Cập nhật lịch sử vận tốc / gia tốc / góc rẽ
    System.ball.update_tracking(Ball, Velocity, now_clock_eval)

    local prev_speed = previous_ball_speeds[Ball] or RawSpeed
    local acceleration = RawSpeed - prev_speed
    previous_ball_speeds[Ball] = RawSpeed

    local future_ball_pos = Ball.Position + (Velocity * dt)
    local diff_vector = char_pos - future_ball_pos
    local dist_sq = diff_vector:Dot(diff_vector)

    if dist_sq <= 0 or dist_sq > 250000 then return end

    local Distance = m_sqrt(dist_sq)
    local prev_dist = previous_ball_distances[Ball] or Distance
    local distance_jump = prev_dist - Distance
    previous_ball_distances[Ball] = Distance

    local speed_buffer = math_max(8, RawSpeed * 0.05)
    local expected_jump = (RawSpeed * dt * 2) + speed_buffer
    local is_blinking = (distance_jump > expected_jump) and Distance < 80

    local inv_mag = 1 / math_max(RelativeSpeed, 1)
    local vel_unit = RelativeVelocity * inv_mag
    local direction = diff_vector / Distance
    local dot_product = direction:Dot(vel_unit)

    -- Khi đang clash, góc dot có thể nhiễu, hạ xuống 0 thay vì 0.1
    if dot_product < 0 then return end

    local closest_approach, distance_along_path =
        GetTrajectoryProjection(future_ball_pos, RelativeVelocity, char_pos)

    local tti = (distance_along_path > 0) and (distance_along_path * inv_mag) or (Distance * inv_mag)

    local dynamic_hitbox = math_max(16 + (RawSpeed * 0.025) + 1, 1)
    local angle_penalty = (1 - math_max(dot_product, 0)) * 0.4
    local distance_penalty = Distance / 2500
    local collision_penalty = (closest_approach / dynamic_hitbox) * 0.3

    local current_score = tti * (1 + angle_penalty) * (1 + collision_penalty) + distance_penalty

    local is_better_threat = false
    if not best_threat.ball then
        is_better_threat = true
    else
        local in_critical_zone = (tti < CRITICAL_TTI) or (best_threat.tti < CRITICAL_TTI)
        if in_critical_zone then
            if current_score < best_threat.score then is_better_threat = true end
        else
            if current_score < best_threat.score * 0.90 then is_better_threat = true end
        end
    end

    if is_better_threat then
        best_threat.ball               = Ball
        best_threat.distance           = Distance
        best_threat.speed              = RawSpeed
        best_threat.relative_speed     = RelativeSpeed
        best_threat.dot                = dot_product
        best_threat.tti                = tti
        best_threat.target             = current_target
        best_threat.is_training        = is_training
        best_threat.velocity           = Velocity
        best_threat.acceleration       = acceleration
        best_threat.projected_distance = closest_approach
        best_threat.is_blinking        = is_blinking
        best_threat.score              = current_score
    end
end

local function perform_autoparry_check(dt)
    dt = type(dt) == "number" and dt or 0.016
    if not System.__properties.__autoparry_enabled then return end

    -- Zenthra: cập nhật forcefield state trước khi check inhibit
    update_forcefield_state()

    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local root_part = character and character.PrimaryPart
    if is_player_inhibited(character, root_part) then return end

    local now_clock = o_clock()
    update_ping_data(now_clock)

    -- Giảm combo của các bóng nếu quá 0.3s không nảy thêm
    local combo_decay = System.__properties.__ball_combo_decay_queue
    if combo_decay then
        for ball, queue in pairs(combo_decay) do
            for i = #queue, 1, -1 do
                if now_clock >= queue[i] then
                    table.remove(queue, i)
                    local current = System.__properties.__ball_parry_count[ball] or 0
                    System.__properties.__ball_parry_count[ball] = math.max(0, current - 1)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local all_balls = System.ball.get_all()
    local training_folder = workspace:FindFirstChild("TrainingBalls")
    local has_training = false
    if training_folder then
        for _, child in ipairs(training_folder:GetChildren()) do
            if child and child:GetAttribute("realBall") then
                table.insert(all_balls, child)
                has_training = true
            end
        end
    end

    if #all_balls == 0 and not has_training then return end

    ResetBestThreat()

    local char_pos = root_part.Position
    local char_vel = root_part.AssemblyLinearVelocity or v_zero
    local local_name = LocalPlayer.Name

    for _, ball in ipairs(all_balls) do
        evaluate_ball_threat(ball, ball.Parent == training_folder, char_pos, char_vel, local_name, dt)
    end

    if not best_threat.ball then return end

    local ping_seconds = smoothedPing / 1000
    local is_curving, is_negative_dot = false, false
    if best_threat.target == local_name and System.detection and System.detection.is_curved then
        is_curving, is_negative_dot = System.detection.is_curved(
            best_threat.ball,
            best_threat.velocity,
            best_threat.speed,
            best_threat.distance,
            char_pos
        )
    end

    if EvaluateParryDecision(best_threat, is_curving, char_pos, ping_seconds, System.__properties, dt, now_clock) then
        if System.animation and System.animation.play_grab_parry then
            System.animation.play_grab_parry()
        end
        if type(getgenv().Parry) == "function" then
            getgenv().Parry()
        end
        if System.spam and System.spam.register_parry then
            System.spam.register_parry(best_threat.ball)
        end
    end
end

System.autoparry.start = function()
    System.autoparry.stop()
    System.__properties.__autoparry_enabled    = true
    System.__properties.__last_parry_time      = 0
    System.__properties.__cooldown_expiration  = 0
    System.__properties.__ball_targets         = System.__properties.__ball_targets or {}
    System.__properties.__interaction_locked   = System.__properties.__interaction_locked or {}
    System.__properties.__fatal_locks          = {}
    System.__properties.__ball_parry_count     = System.__properties.__ball_parry_count or {}
    System.__properties.__ball_combo_decay_queue = System.__properties.__ball_combo_decay_queue or {}
    System.__properties.__connections          = System.__properties.__connections or {}

    -- [ZENTHRA AUTHENTIC: DUAL-SIMULATION PHASE]
    local function on_autoparry_step(dt)
        if System.__properties.__autoparry_enabled then
            perform_autoparry_check(dt)
        end
    end

    System.__properties.__connections.__ap_presim = RunService.PreSimulation:Connect(on_autoparry_step)
    System.__properties.__connections.__ap_postsim = RunService.PostSimulation:Connect(on_autoparry_step)

    -- 40 CPS Clash Engine tự khởi động cùng Auto-Parry
    if System.clash_engine then System.clash_engine.start() end
end

System.autoparry.stop = function()
    System.__properties.__autoparry_enabled   = false
    System.__properties.__last_parry_time     = 0
    System.__properties.__cooldown_expiration = 0
    System.__properties.__ball_targets        = {}
    System.__properties.__interaction_locked  = {}
    System.__properties.__fatal_locks         = {}
    System.__properties.__ball_parry_count    = {}
    System.__properties.__ball_combo_decay_queue = {}

    if System.__properties.__connections then
        if System.__properties.__connections.__ap_presim then
            pcall(function() System.__properties.__connections.__ap_presim:Disconnect() end)
            System.__properties.__connections.__ap_presim = nil
        end
        if System.__properties.__connections.__ap_postsim then
            pcall(function() System.__properties.__connections.__ap_postsim:Disconnect() end)
            System.__properties.__connections.__ap_postsim = nil
        end
    end

    -- 40 CPS Clash Engine tự tắt cùng Auto-Parry
    if System.clash_engine then System.clash_engine.stop() end

    table.clear(previous_ball_speeds)
    table.clear(previous_ball_distances)
end

System.clash_engine = {
    enabled = false,
    _running = false,
    _conn_pre = nil,
    _conn_post = nil,

    -- State riêng biệt (không dùng chung với ai)
    _last_fire_tick = 0,
    _parries = 0,
    _last_parried_ball = nil,
    _decay_queue = {},
    _cooldown = 0.0125, -- 80 CPS = 12.5ms giữa mỗi nhịp
}

local CE = System.clash_engine

local function clash_engine_fire_parry()
    -- [PHYSICS UPDATE BYPASS]: Kỹ thuật ép Ping-Pong server
    pcall(function()
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hrp = char and (char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char.PrimaryPart)
        if hrp then
            hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 0.01, 0)
        end
    end)

    if type(getgenv().Parry) == "function" then
        getgenv().Parry()
    else
        local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        if remotes then
            local pr = remotes:FindFirstChild("ParryButtonPress") or remotes:FindFirstChild("ParryAttempt")
            if pr then pr:FireServer() end
        end
    end
end

local function clash_engine_step()
    if not CE.enabled then return end
    if not System.__properties.__autoparry_enabled then return end

    -- [USER REQUEST: MUTUAL EXCLUSION] 
    -- Nếu Auto Spam (hay Manual Spam) đang bật thì Clash Engine 80cps phải tạm nghỉ!
    if System.__properties.__auto_spam_enabled or System.__properties.__manual_spam_enabled then return end

    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    local root = char and char.PrimaryPart
    if not root then return end

    local now = o_clock()

    -- Tự quản lý decay queue (mỗi parry tự hết hạn sau 0.2s)
    local dq = CE._decay_queue
    for i = #dq, 1, -1 do
        if now >= dq[i] then
            table.remove(dq, i)
            CE._parries = math_max(0, CE._parries - 1)
        end
    end

    -- Cooldown riêng: 80 CPS = 12.5ms
    if now - CE._last_fire_tick < CE._cooldown then return end

    -- Tìm bóng đang nhắm vào mình
    local my_name = LocalPlayer.Name
    local balls_folder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Balls")
    if not balls_folder then return end

    local root_pos = root.Position

    for _, ball in ipairs(balls_folder:GetChildren()) do
        local part = ball:GetAttribute("realBall") and ball
            or ball:IsA("Model") and (ball:FindFirstChild("Corpo") or ball.PrimaryPart or ball:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart"))
            or ball:IsA("BasePart") and ball
            or nil

        if part and part.Parent then
            local target = ball:GetAttribute("target")
            local dist = (part.Position - root_pos).Magnitude
            local is_last_parried = (CE._last_parried_ball == part and dist <= 30)

            -- Chỉ xử lý nếu: bóng nhắm vào mình HOẶC vừa clash gần (<= 30 studs)
            if target == my_name or is_last_parried then
                local zoomies = ball:FindFirstChild("zoomies")
                local vel = zoomies and zoomies.VectorVelocity or (part.AssemblyLinearVelocity or v_zero)
                local speed = vel.Magnitude

                if speed < 25 then continue end

                -- Tính TTI đơn giản
                local simple_tti = dist / math_max(speed, 1)

                -- Tính cự ly linh hoạt (tối đa 35 studs)
                local ping_s = (smoothedPing or 50) * 0.001
                local network_delay = (ping_s / 1.3) + 0.03
                local frame_travel = speed / 60
                local hitbox_radius = 13.5 + (speed * 0.02)
                local ping_factor = math_clamp((smoothedPing or 50) / 10, 1, 15)
                local k = 2.4 + math_min(math_max(speed - 9.5, 0), 650) * 0.002
                local base_range = ping_factor + math_max(speed / k, 9.5)

                local flexible_clash_dist = math_clamp(
                    hitbox_radius + (frame_travel * 0.75) + (network_delay * speed * 0.35),
                    12,
                    math_min(base_range * 0.65, 35)
                )

                -- Hướng bóng đang tiến lại
                local diff = root_pos - part.Position
                local diff_mag = diff.Magnitude
                if diff_mag <= 0 then continue end
                local dir = diff / diff_mag
                local dot = dir:Dot(vel.Magnitude > 0 and (vel / vel.Magnitude) or v_zero)
                local is_approaching = dot > 0.10

                -- Kiểm tra curve (nếu có)
                local is_curving = false
                if System.detection and System.detection.is_curved then
                    local ok, cv = pcall(System.detection.is_curved, ball, vel, speed, dist, root_pos)
                    if ok then is_curving = cv end
                end

                local current_combo = System.__properties.__ball_parry_count[ball] or 0
                local req_combo = System.__properties.__spam_threshold or 3

                -- ĐỦ HẾT điều kiện mới kích hoạt (AND STRICT 100%)
                local eligible = (simple_tti <= 0.225)
                    and (dist <= flexible_clash_dist)
                    and is_approaching
                    and (current_combo >= req_combo)
                    and (not is_curving) -- DỪNG HẲN SPAM NẾU CÓ CURVE

                if eligible then
                    CE._last_fire_tick = now
                    CE._parries = CE._parries + 1
                    CE._last_parried_ball = part
                    CE._decay_queue[#CE._decay_queue + 1] = now + 0.2

                    clash_engine_fire_parry()
                    break -- Chỉ parry 1 quả bóng mỗi tick
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

CE.start = function()
    if CE._running then return end
    CE.enabled = true
    CE._running = true
    CE._parries = 0
    CE._last_parried_ball = nil
    CE._decay_queue = {}
    CE._last_fire_tick = 0

    task.spawn(function()
        local PreSim = RunService.PreSimulation
        local PostSim = RunService.PostSimulation

        while CE.enabled do
            clash_engine_step()
            PostSim:Wait()

            if not CE.enabled then break end

            clash_engine_step()
            PreSim:Wait()
        end

        CE._running = false
    end)
end

CE.stop = function()
    CE.enabled = false
    CE._running = false
    CE._parries = 0
    CE._last_parried_ball = nil
    CE._decay_queue = {}
end



-- ==============================================================
-- 1. ZENTHRA EVENT-DRIVEN TRIGGERBOT
-- ==============================================================
System.triggerbot = {
    enabled = false,
    delay_ms = 0,
    range = 35,
    ball_conns = {}
}

System.triggerbot.start = function()
    System.triggerbot.stop()
    System.triggerbot.enabled = true

    local function attach_ball(ball)
        if not ball or not ball:IsA("BasePart") or System.triggerbot.ball_conns[ball] then return end

        local function check_and_fire()
            if not System.triggerbot.enabled or not ball.Parent then return end
            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            local root = char and char.PrimaryPart
            if not root then return end

            local target = ball:GetAttribute("target")
            if target ~= LocalPlayer.Name then return end

            local zoomies = ball:FindFirstChild("zoomies")
            local speed = zoomies and zoomies.VectorVelocity.Magnitude or (ball.AssemblyLinearVelocity and ball.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude or 50)
            local dist = (root.Position - ball.Position).Magnitude

            local ping_val = smoothedPing or 50
            local ping_factor = math.clamp(ping_val / 10, 1, 15)
            local clamped_speed = math.clamp(speed - 9.5, 0, 650)
            local speed_div = 2.4 + (clamped_speed * 0.002)
            local hit_range = ping_factor + math.max(speed / speed_div, 9.5)

            if dist <= hit_range then
                if System.triggerbot.delay_ms > 0 then
                    task.delay(System.triggerbot.delay_ms / 1000, function()
                        if type(getgenv().Parry) == "function" then getgenv().Parry() end
                    end)
                else
                    if type(getgenv().Parry) == "function" then getgenv().Parry() end
                end
            end
        end

        local conn = ball:GetAttributeChangedSignal("target"):Connect(check_and_fire)
        System.triggerbot.ball_conns[ball] = conn
    end

    for _, b in ipairs(System.ball.get_all()) do attach_ball(b) end
    local balls_folder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Balls")
    if balls_folder then
        System.triggerbot.child_conn = balls_folder.ChildAdded:Connect(attach_ball)
    end
end

System.triggerbot.stop = function()
    System.triggerbot.enabled = false
    for _, conn in pairs(System.triggerbot.ball_conns) do
        pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
    end
    table.clear(System.triggerbot.ball_conns)
    if System.triggerbot.child_conn then
        pcall(function() System.triggerbot.child_conn:Disconnect() end)
        System.triggerbot.child_conn = nil
    end
end

-- ==============================================================
-- 2. HIGH-VISIBILITY 3D PARRY VISUALIZER (MOBILE + PC COMPATIBLE)
-- ==============================================================
System.visualizer = {
    enabled = true,
    color_mode = "Neon Cyan",
    vis_type = "3D Ring",
    parts = {},
    folder = nil,
    sphere_part = nil,
    adornment = nil,
    segments = 64,
    thickness = 0.45,
    height = 0.45,
    conn = nil
}

local function destroy_visualizer_parts()
    if System.visualizer.conn then
        pcall(function() System.visualizer.conn:Disconnect() end)
        System.visualizer.conn = nil
    end
    for _, p in ipairs(System.visualizer.parts) do
        pcall(function() p:Destroy() end)
    end
    table.clear(System.visualizer.parts)
    if System.visualizer.sphere_part and System.visualizer.sphere_part.Parent then
        pcall(function() System.visualizer.sphere_part:Destroy() end)
        System.visualizer.sphere_part = nil
    end
    if System.visualizer.adornment and System.visualizer.adornment.Parent then
        pcall(function() System.visualizer.adornment:Destroy() end)
        System.visualizer.adornment = nil
    end
    if System.visualizer.folder and System.visualizer.folder.Parent then
        pcall(function() System.visualizer.folder:Destroy() end)
        System.visualizer.folder = nil
    end
end

local function update_visualizer_ring()
    if not System.visualizer.enabled then
        destroy_visualizer_parts()
        return
    end

    if not System.visualizer.folder or not System.visualizer.folder.Parent then
        local existingFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("ParryVisualizerRing")
        if existingFolder then
            existingFolder:Destroy()
        end
        local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
        folder.Name = "ParryVisualizerRing"
        folder.Parent = workspace
        System.visualizer.folder = folder
    end

    -- Forcefully clear any old sphere/adornments from previous executions
    for _, child in ipairs(System.visualizer.folder:GetChildren()) do
        if child.Name == "ParryVisualizerSphere" or child.Name == "ParryVisualizerAdornment" then
            child:Destroy()
        end
    end

    local segments = System.visualizer.segments or 72
    if #System.visualizer.parts ~= segments then
        for _, p in ipairs(System.visualizer.parts) do
            pcall(function() p:Destroy() end)
        end
        table.clear(System.visualizer.parts)

        for i = 1, segments do
            local part = Instance.new("Part")
            part.Name = "Segment_" .. i
            part.Anchored = true
            part.CanCollide = false
            part.CanQuery = false
            part.CanTouch = false
            part.CastShadow = false
            part.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
            part.TopSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
            part.BottomSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
            part.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 220, 255)
            part.Parent = System.visualizer.folder
            table.insert(System.visualizer.parts, part)
        end
    end

    if not System.visualizer.conn then
        System.visualizer.conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
            if not System.visualizer.enabled then
                destroy_visualizer_parts()
                return
            end

            local character = LocalPlayer.Character
            local root = character and (character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or character.PrimaryPart or character:FindFirstChild("Torso"))
            if not character or not root then
                for _, p in ipairs(System.visualizer.parts) do
                    p.Transparency = 1
                end
                if System.visualizer.sphere_part then
                    System.visualizer.sphere_part.Transparency = 1
                end
                if System.visualizer.adornment then
                    System.visualizer.adornment.Visible = false
                end
                return
            end

            local ping_val = (typeof(GetPing) == "function" and GetPing()) or 50
            local current_ball = (System.ball and System.ball.get and System.ball.get())
            local ball_speed = 50

            if current_ball and current_ball.Parent then
                local zoomies = current_ball:FindFirstChild("zoomies")
                if zoomies and zoomies.VectorVelocity then
                    ball_speed = zoomies.VectorVelocity.Magnitude
                elseif current_ball.AssemblyLinearVelocity then
                    ball_speed = current_ball.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude
                elseif current_ball.Velocity then
                    ball_speed = current_ball.Velocity.Magnitude
                end
            end

            local current_acc = (System.__properties and System.__properties.__accuracy) or 100
            local ping_seconds = ping_val / 1000
            
            -- [DEBUG]: Dùng chung đúng 1 hàm tính toán khoảng cách của Auto Parry
            local radius = 15
            if typeof(get_premium_parry_distance) == "function" then
                radius = get_premium_parry_distance(ball_speed, current_acc, ping_seconds)
            else
                local ping_factor = math.clamp(ping_val / 10, 5, 17)
                local clamped_speed = math.clamp(ball_speed - 9.5, 0, 650)
                local speed_div = (2.4 + (clamped_speed * 0.002)) * (1 + (100 - current_acc) * 0.003)
                radius = ping_factor + math.max(ball_speed / speed_div, 9.5)
            end
            
            -- Trích xuất Dot Product y như Auto Parry để cộng Dot Boost
            local center = root.Position - Vector3.new(0, 2.0, 0)
            if current_ball and current_ball.Parent and current_ball:FindFirstChild("zoomies") then
                local vel = current_ball.zoomies.VectorVelocity
                if vel.Magnitude > 0 then
                    local dir = (root.Position - current_ball.Position).Unit
                    local dot = dir:Dot(vel.Unit)
                    local dot_boost = (dot > 0) and (dot * 3.0) or 0
                    radius = radius + dot_boost
                end
            end
            
            -- [DYNAMIC CLAMP]: Giới hạn bán kính tối đa hiển thị theo Parry Range Multiplier
            local visual_range_mult = math.clamp(tonumber(System.__properties and System.__properties.__parry_range_multiplier) or 1, 0.1, 4)
            local max_visual_radius = 35 * visual_range_mult
            radius = math.clamp(radius, 6, max_visual_radius)
            
            -- [SMOOTH UPGRADE]: Asymmetric Lerp (Chống giật khi đánh Ping-Pong)
            System.visualizer.current_radius = System.visualizer.current_radius or radius
            local current = System.visualizer.current_radius
            if radius > current then
                -- Nở ra cực nhanh để phản hồi ngay lập tức lúc bóng lao tới
                current = current + (radius - current) * math.clamp(25 * dt, 0, 1)
            else
                -- Thu lại ở tốc độ trung bình (10 * dt) theo yêu cầu của sếp
                current = current + (radius - current) * math.clamp(10 * dt, 0, 1)
            end
            System.visualizer.current_radius = current
            local smooth_radius = current

            local t = os.clock()

            local currentColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 220, 255)
            if System.visualizer.color_mode == "Rainbow RGB" then
                currentColor = Color3.fromHSV((t * 0.4) % 1, 0.85, 1)
            elseif System.visualizer.color_mode == "Theme Pink" then
                currentColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 105, 180)
            elseif System.visualizer.color_mode == "Golden" then
                currentColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 50)
            elseif System.visualizer.color_mode == "Crimson" then
                currentColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 40, 60)
            end

            -- Update 3D Neon Segments (Ultra Smooth)
                local seg_count = #System.visualizer.parts
                if seg_count > 0 then
                    local circle_circumference = 2 * math.pi * smooth_radius / seg_count
                    
                    -- [SMOOTH UPGRADE]: Nhịp đập (Pulse Effect) cho độ dày của vòng
                    local pulse = math.sin(t * 6) * 0.05
                    local thick = (System.visualizer.thickness or 0.45) + pulse
                    local hgt = (System.visualizer.height or 0.45) + pulse

                    for i, part in ipairs(System.visualizer.parts) do
                        local angle = (i - 1) / seg_count * math.pi * 2
                        local offsetX = math.cos(angle) * smooth_radius
                        local offsetZ = math.sin(angle) * smooth_radius
                        
                        local pos1 = center + Vector3.new(offsetX, 0, offsetZ)
                        local pos2 = center + Vector3.new(offsetX - math.sin(angle), 0, offsetZ + math.cos(angle))

                        part.Size = Vector3.new(thick, hgt, circle_circumference + 0.08)
                        part.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos1, pos2)
                        part.Color = currentColor
                        part.Transparency = 0.15
                        part.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
                    end
                end
        end)
    end
end

-- ==============================================================
-- 3. ZENTHRA ORIGINAL BALL TRAIL (AUTHENTIC MATH SPIRAL)
-- ==============================================================
local ZenthraBallTrail = {
    enabled = false,
    num_trails = 4,
    color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 220, 255),
    texture = "rbxassetid://5029929719",
    rainbow = true,
    attachments = {},
    conn = nil
}

local function clear_ball_trail_attachments()
    for _, item in ipairs(ZenthraBallTrail.attachments) do
        if item.a0 and item.a0.Parent then item.a0:Destroy() end
        if item.a1 and item.a1.Parent then item.a1:Destroy() end
        if item.trail and item.trail.Parent then item.trail:Destroy() end
    end
    table.clear(ZenthraBallTrail.attachments)
end

local function setup_ball_trail(ball)
    clear_ball_trail_attachments()
    if not ZenthraBallTrail.enabled or not ball or not ball.Parent then return end

    local num = ZenthraBallTrail.num_trails or 4
    for i = 1, num do
        local p = (i / num) * math.pi * 2
        local x_radius = math.random(150, 250) / 100
        local y_height = math.random(-150, 150) / 100

        local a0 = Instance.new("Attachment")
        a0.Position = Vector3.new(math.cos(p) * x_radius, y_height, math.sin(p) * x_radius)
        a0.Parent = ball

        local a1 = Instance.new("Attachment")
        a1.Position = Vector3.new(math.cos(p + math.pi * 0.7) * x_radius * 1.3, -y_height, math.sin(p + math.pi * 0.7) * x_radius * 1.3)
        a1.Parent = ball

        local trail = Instance.new("Trail")
        trail.Attachment0 = a0
        trail.Attachment1 = a1
        trail.Lifetime = 0.6
        trail.MinLength = 0
        trail.FaceCamera = true
        trail.LightEmission = 1
        trail.LightInfluence = 0
        trail.Texture = ZenthraBallTrail.texture
        trail.TextureMode = Enum.TextureMode.Stretch

        local col = ZenthraBallTrail.color
        if ZenthraBallTrail.rainbow then
            col = Color3.fromHSV((os.clock() * 0.5) % 1, 0.9, 1)
        end
        trail.Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, col),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.new(math.min(col.R * 1.3, 1), math.min(col.G * 1.3, 1), math.min(col.B * 1.3, 1))),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, col)
        })

        trail.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.2),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.3, 0),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.7, 0.3),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
        })
        trail.WidthScale = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.1),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.3, 0.25),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.7, 0.15),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.02),
        })
        trail.Parent = ball

        table.insert(ZenthraBallTrail.attachments, {
            a0 = a0,
            a1 = a1,
            trail = trail,
            baseAngle = p,
            angle = 0,
            speed = math.random(15, 30) / 10,
            spiralSpeed = math.random(25, 45) / 10,
            radiusMultiplier = math.random(80, 130) / 100,
            pulseOffset = math.random() * math.pi * 2,
            baseRadius = x_radius,
            baseHeight = y_height,
            chaosSpeed = math.random(10, 20) / 10,
        })
    end
end

local function update_ball_trail_step(dt)
    if not ZenthraBallTrail.enabled then
        clear_ball_trail_attachments()
        return
    end

    local ball = System.ball and System.ball.get and System.ball.get()
    if not ball or not ball.Parent then
        clear_ball_trail_attachments()
        return
    end

    if #ZenthraBallTrail.attachments == 0 then
        setup_ball_trail(ball)
        return
    end

    local now = os.clock()
    local currentColor = ZenthraBallTrail.color
    if ZenthraBallTrail.rainbow then
        currentColor = Color3.fromHSV((now * 0.5) % 1, 0.9, 1)
    end
    local colorSeq = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, currentColor),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.new(math.min(currentColor.R * 1.3, 1), math.min(currentColor.G * 1.3, 1), math.min(currentColor.B * 1.3, 1))),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, currentColor)
    })

    for _, item in ipairs(ZenthraBallTrail.attachments) do
        if not item.a0 or not item.a0.Parent or not item.a1 or not item.a1.Parent then
            clear_ball_trail_attachments()
            return
        end

        item.angle = item.angle + item.speed * dt

        local spiralAngle = item.angle * item.spiralSpeed
        local pulse = math.sin(now * 4 + item.pulseOffset) * 0.4 + 1
        local wave = math.sin(item.angle * 3) * 0.7
        local chaos = math.sin(now * item.chaosSpeed + item.pulseOffset) * 0.5
        local radius0 = item.baseRadius * item.radiusMultiplier * pulse
        local radius1 = item.baseRadius * 1.3 * item.radiusMultiplier * pulse

        item.a0.Position = Vector3.new(
            math.cos(item.baseAngle + item.angle) * radius0 + math.cos(spiralAngle) * 0.6,
            item.baseHeight + math.sin((item.baseAngle + item.angle) * 3) * 0.8 + wave + chaos + math.sin(spiralAngle * 2) * 0.6,
            math.sin(item.baseAngle + item.angle) * radius0 + math.sin(spiralAngle) * 0.6
        )

        item.a1.Position = Vector3.new(
            math.cos(item.baseAngle + item.angle + math.pi * 0.7) * radius1 + math.sin(spiralAngle * 1.3) * 0.5,
            -item.baseHeight + math.cos((item.baseAngle + item.angle) * 2.5) * 0.8 - wave - chaos + math.cos(spiralAngle * 1.7) * 0.5,
            math.sin(item.baseAngle + item.angle + math.pi * 0.7) * radius1 + math.cos(spiralAngle * 1.1) * 0.5
        )

        if item.trail and item.trail.Parent then
            item.trail.LightEmission = math.sin(now * 5 + item.pulseOffset) * 0.4 + 0.6
            item.trail.Color = colorSeq
        end
    end
end

System.orbit_ball = {
    enabled = false,
    distance = 6,
    height = 2,
    speed = 2.2,
    mode = "JJK Hollow Purple",
    folder = nil,
    start_time = 0,
    red_part = nil,
    blue_part = nil,
    purple_part = nil,
    shockwave_part = nil,
    red_attachments = {},
    blue_attachments = {},
    purple_attachments = {},
    conn = nil
}

local function clear_orbit_attachments(list)
    for _, item in ipairs(list) do
        if item.a0 and item.a0.Parent then item.a0:Destroy() end
        if item.a1 and item.a1.Parent then item.a1:Destroy() end
        if item.trail and item.trail.Parent then item.trail:Destroy() end
    end
    table.clear(list)
end

local function destroy_orbit_ball()
    if System.orbit_ball.conn then
        pcall(function() System.orbit_ball.conn:Disconnect() end)
        System.orbit_ball.conn = nil
    end
    clear_orbit_attachments(System.orbit_ball.red_attachments)
    clear_orbit_attachments(System.orbit_ball.blue_attachments)
    clear_orbit_attachments(System.orbit_ball.purple_attachments)
    if System.orbit_ball.folder and System.orbit_ball.folder.Parent then
        pcall(function() System.orbit_ball.folder:Destroy() end)
        System.orbit_ball.folder = nil
    end
    System.orbit_ball.red_part = nil
    System.orbit_ball.blue_part = nil
    System.orbit_ball.purple_part = nil
    System.orbit_ball.shockwave_part = nil
end

local function create_orbit_spiral_trails(parentPart, color1, color2, color3, numTrails, targetList, textureId)
    clear_orbit_attachments(targetList)
    if not parentPart or not parentPart.Parent then return end

    local tex = textureId or "rbxassetid://5029929719"
    for i = 1, numTrails do
        local p = (i / numTrails) * math.pi * 2
        local baseR = 1.3
        local baseH = 0.6

        local a0 = Instance.new("Attachment")
        a0.Position = Vector3.new(math.cos(p) * baseR, baseH, math.sin(p) * baseR)
        a0.Parent = parentPart

        local a1 = Instance.new("Attachment")
        a1.Position = Vector3.new(math.cos(p + math.pi * 0.7) * baseR * 1.3, -baseH, math.sin(p + math.pi * 0.7) * baseR * 1.3)
        a1.Parent = parentPart

        local trail = Instance.new("Trail")
        trail.Attachment0 = a0
        trail.Attachment1 = a1
        trail.Lifetime = 0.6
        trail.MinLength = 0
        trail.FaceCamera = true
        trail.LightEmission = 1
        trail.LightInfluence = 0
        trail.Texture = tex
        trail.TextureMode = Enum.TextureMode.Stretch

        trail.Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, color1),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, color2),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, color3)
        })

        trail.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.1),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.3, 0),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.7, 0.3),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
        })
        trail.WidthScale = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.15),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4, 0.35),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.8, 0.18),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.02)
        })
        trail.Parent = parentPart

        table.insert(targetList, {
            a0 = a0,
            a1 = a1,
            trail = trail,
            baseAngle = p,
            angle = 0,
            speed = 2.5 + (i * 0.6),
            spiralSpeed = 3.5 + (i * 0.8),
            pulseOffset = i * (math.pi / 2),
            baseRadius = baseR,
            baseHeight = baseH,
            chaosSpeed = 1.5 + (i * 0.4)
        })
    end
end

local function update_orbit_spiral_step(targetList, now, dt, ballScale)
    ballScale = ballScale or 1.0
    for _, item in ipairs(targetList) do
        if item.a0 and item.a0.Parent and item.a1 and item.a1.Parent then
            item.angle = item.angle + item.speed * dt
            local spiralAngle = item.angle * item.spiralSpeed
            local pulse = math.sin(now * 5 + item.pulseOffset) * 0.35 + 1
            local wave = math.sin(item.angle * 3) * 0.5 * ballScale
            local chaos = math.sin(now * item.chaosSpeed + item.pulseOffset) * 0.4 * ballScale
            local r0 = item.baseRadius * ballScale * pulse
            local r1 = item.baseRadius * 1.35 * ballScale * pulse

            item.a0.Position = Vector3.new(
                math.cos(item.baseAngle + item.angle) * r0 + math.cos(spiralAngle) * (0.35 * ballScale),
                item.baseHeight * ballScale + wave + chaos,
                math.sin(item.baseAngle + item.angle) * r0 + math.sin(spiralAngle) * (0.35 * ballScale)
            )

            item.a1.Position = Vector3.new(
                math.cos(item.baseAngle + item.angle + math.pi * 0.7) * r1 + math.sin(spiralAngle * 1.2) * (0.3 * ballScale),
                -item.baseHeight * ballScale - wave - chaos,
                math.sin(item.baseAngle + item.angle + math.pi * 0.7) * r1 + math.cos(spiralAngle * 1.2) * (0.3 * ballScale)
            )

            if item.trail and item.trail.Parent then
                item.trail.LightEmission = math.sin(now * 5 + item.pulseOffset) * 0.3 + 0.7
            end
        end
    end
end

local function setup_orbit_ball()
    destroy_orbit_ball()
    if not System.orbit_ball.enabled then return end

    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return end

    local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
    folder.Name = "JJK_HollowPurple_Orbit"
    folder.Parent = workspace
    System.orbit_ball.folder = folder
    System.orbit_ball.start_time = os.clock()

    -- 1. RED ORB (CỰC THUẬN "XÍCH")
    local redSphere = Instance.new("Part")
    redSphere.Name = "JJK_Red_Orb"
    redSphere.Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball
    redSphere.Size = Vector3.new(1.4, 1.4, 1.4)
    redSphere.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
    redSphere.CanCollide = false
    redSphere.Anchored = true
    redSphere.CastShadow = false
    redSphere.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 30, 50)
    redSphere.Parent = folder

    create_orbit_spiral_trails(
        redSphere,
        Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 50),
        Color3.fromRGB(255, 20, 40),
        Color3.fromRGB(150, 0, 0),
        2,
        System.orbit_ball.red_attachments,
        "rbxassetid://5029929719"
    )

    -- 2. BLUE ORB (CỰC NGHỊCH "THƯƠNG")
    local blueSphere = Instance.new("Part")
    blueSphere.Name = "JJK_Blue_Orb"
    blueSphere.Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball
    blueSphere.Size = Vector3.new(1.4, 1.4, 1.4)
    blueSphere.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
    blueSphere.CanCollide = false
    blueSphere.Anchored = true
    blueSphere.CastShadow = false
    blueSphere.Color = Color3.fromRGB(30, 160, 255)
    blueSphere.Transparency = 1
    blueSphere.Parent = folder

    create_orbit_spiral_trails(
        blueSphere,
        Color3.fromRGB(100, 230, 255),
        Color3.fromRGB(0, 140, 255),
        Color3.fromRGB(0, 30, 180),
        2,
        System.orbit_ball.blue_attachments,
        "rbxassetid://7332766373"
    )

    -- 3. HOLLOW PURPLE (HƯ THỨC "TỬ")
    local purpleSphere = Instance.new("Part")
    purpleSphere.Name = "JJK_Hollow_Purple_Orb"
    purpleSphere.Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball
    purpleSphere.Size = Vector3.new(2.8, 2.8, 2.8)
    purpleSphere.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
    purpleSphere.CanCollide = false
    purpleSphere.Anchored = true
    purpleSphere.CastShadow = false
    purpleSphere.Color = Color3.fromRGB(190, 40, 255)
    purpleSphere.Transparency = 1
    purpleSphere.Parent = folder

    create_orbit_spiral_trails(
        purpleSphere,
        Color3.fromRGB(255, 170, 255),
        Color3.fromRGB(180, 20, 255),
        Color3.fromRGB(75, 0, 150),
        4,
        System.orbit_ball.purple_attachments,
        "rbxassetid://3069698305"
    )

    System.orbit_ball.red_part = redSphere
    System.orbit_ball.blue_part = blueSphere
    System.orbit_ball.purple_part = purpleSphere

    System.orbit_ball.conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
        if not System.orbit_ball.enabled or not System.orbit_ball.folder or not System.orbit_ball.folder.Parent then
            destroy_orbit_ball()
            return
        end

        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        local root = character and (character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or character.PrimaryPart or character:FindFirstChild("Torso"))
        if not character or not root then
            if System.orbit_ball.red_part then System.orbit_ball.red_part.Transparency = 1 end
            if System.orbit_ball.blue_part then System.orbit_ball.blue_part.Transparency = 1 end
            if System.orbit_ball.purple_part then System.orbit_ball.purple_part.Transparency = 1 end
            return
        end

        local rootPos = root.Position
        local rootCFrame = root.CFrame
        local dist = System.orbit_ball.distance or 6
        local height = System.orbit_ball.height or 2
        local speed = System.orbit_ball.speed or 2.2
        local now = os.clock()

        local elapsed = now - (System.orbit_ball.start_time or now)
        local cycleLength = 33.0
        local cycleTime = (elapsed % cycleLength)
        local baseAngle = elapsed * speed

        -- Non-JJK Color Modes
        if System.orbit_ball.mode ~= "JJK Hollow Purple" then
            blueSphere.Transparency = 1
            purpleSphere.Transparency = 1
            redSphere.Transparency = 0

            local customColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 220, 255)
            if System.orbit_ball.mode == "Rainbow RGB" then
                customColor = Color3.fromHSV((now * 0.4) % 1, 0.85, 1)
            elseif System.orbit_ball.mode == "Neon Cyan" then
                customColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 220, 255)
            elseif System.orbit_ball.mode == "Crimson Red" then
                customColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 30, 50)
            elseif System.orbit_ball.mode == "Electric Blue" then
                customColor = Color3.fromRGB(30, 160, 255)
            elseif System.orbit_ball.mode == "Cosmic Purple" then
                customColor = Color3.fromRGB(190, 40, 255)
            elseif System.orbit_ball.mode == "Golden Star" then
                customColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 50)
            end

            redSphere.Color = customColor
            update_orbit_spiral_step(System.orbit_ball.red_attachments, now, dt, 1.0)

            local rX = rootPos.X + math.cos(baseAngle) * dist
            local rZ = rootPos.Z + math.sin(baseAngle) * dist
            local rY = rootPos.Y + height + math.sin(now * 3) * 0.7
            redSphere.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.new(rX, rY, rZ), rootPos)
            return
        end

        if cycleTime < 15.0 then
            
            redSphere.Transparency = 0
            blueSphere.Transparency = 1
            purpleSphere.Transparency = 1

            local rX = rootPos.X + math.cos(baseAngle) * dist
            local rZ = rootPos.Z + math.sin(baseAngle) * dist
            local rY = rootPos.Y + height + math.sin(now * 3) * 0.7
            redSphere.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.new(rX, rY, rZ), rootPos)

            update_orbit_spiral_step(System.orbit_ball.red_attachments, now, dt, 1.0)

        elseif cycleTime < 21.0 then
           
            local p = (cycleTime - 15.0) / 6.0
            redSphere.Transparency = 0
            blueSphere.Transparency = 0
            purpleSphere.Transparency = 1

            -- Red continues smooth orbit
            local rX = rootPos.X + math.cos(baseAngle) * dist
            local rZ = rootPos.Z + math.sin(baseAngle) * dist
            local rY = rootPos.Y + height + math.sin(now * 3) * 0.7
            redSphere.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.new(rX, rY, rZ), rootPos)

            -- Blue target position opposite to Red
            local targetBlueAngle = baseAngle + math.pi
            local bTargetX = rootPos.X + math.cos(targetBlueAngle) * dist
            local bTargetZ = rootPos.Z + math.sin(targetBlueAngle) * dist
            local bTargetY = rootPos.Y + height + math.cos(now * 3) * 0.7
            local targetBluePos = Vector3.new(bTargetX, bTargetY, bTargetZ)

            -- Spawn smoothly 35 studs behind player
            local spawnBackPos = rootPos - (rootCFrame.LookVector * 35) + Vector3.new(0, height + 4, 0)
            local currentBluePos = spawnBackPos:Lerp(targetBluePos, math.clamp(p * 1.8, 0, 1))

            blueSphere.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(currentBluePos, rootPos)

            update_orbit_spiral_step(System.orbit_ball.red_attachments, now, dt, 1.0)
            update_orbit_spiral_step(System.orbit_ball.blue_attachments, now, dt, 1.0)

        elseif cycleTime < 25.0 then
           
            local p = (cycleTime - 21.0) / 4.0
            local fadeAlpha = math.clamp(p * 1.4, 0, 1)
            redSphere.Transparency = fadeAlpha
            blueSphere.Transparency = fadeAlpha
            purpleSphere.Transparency = math.clamp(1 - fadeAlpha * 1.2, 0, 1)

            local spiralDist = dist * (1 - p)
            local spiralSpin = baseAngle + (elapsed * speed * 3.5 * p)
            local fusionCenter = rootPos + (rootCFrame.LookVector * 3.5) + Vector3.new(0, height, 0)

            local rX = fusionCenter.X + math.cos(spiralSpin) * spiralDist
            local rZ = fusionCenter.Z + math.sin(spiralSpin) * spiralDist
            local rY = fusionCenter.Y + math.sin(spiralSpin * 3) * (0.8 * (1 - p))
            redSphere.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.new(rX, rY, rZ), fusionCenter)

            local bX = fusionCenter.X + math.cos(spiralSpin + math.pi) * spiralDist
            local bZ = fusionCenter.Z + math.sin(spiralSpin + math.pi) * spiralDist
            local bY = fusionCenter.Y - math.sin(spiralSpin * 3) * (0.8 * (1 - p))
            blueSphere.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.new(bX, bY, bZ), fusionCenter)

            purpleSphere.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(fusionCenter, rootPos)
            local pSize = 1.6 + (p * 1.2)
            purpleSphere.Size = Vector3.new(pSize, pSize, pSize)

            update_orbit_spiral_step(System.orbit_ball.red_attachments, now, dt, 1.0 - (p * 0.5))
            update_orbit_spiral_step(System.orbit_ball.blue_attachments, now, dt, 1.0 - (p * 0.5))
            update_orbit_spiral_step(System.orbit_ball.purple_attachments, now, dt, p * 1.2)

        else
            
            redSphere.Transparency = 1
            blueSphere.Transparency = 1

            local purpleSubTime = cycleTime - 25.0

            if purpleSubTime < 4.5 then
                -- 4A: Cosmic Orbit & Power Surge (25s - 29.5s)
                purpleSphere.Transparency = 0
                local pulse = 2.8 + math.sin(now * 12) * 0.45
                purpleSphere.Size = Vector3.new(pulse, pulse, pulse)

                local pAngle = elapsed * speed * 1.2
                local pX = rootPos.X + math.cos(pAngle) * (dist * 0.85)
                local pZ = rootPos.Z + math.sin(pAngle) * (dist * 0.85)
                local pY = rootPos.Y + height + math.sin(now * 4) * 0.9
                purpleSphere.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.new(pX, pY, pZ), rootPos)
                purpleSphere.Color = Color3.fromRGB(190, 40, 255)

                update_orbit_spiral_step(System.orbit_ball.purple_attachments, now, dt, 1.4)

            elseif purpleSubTime < 5.5 then
                -- 4B: Lock Aim In Front of Player & Overcharge (29.5s - 30.5s)
                local lockP = (purpleSubTime - 4.5) / 1.0
                purpleSphere.Transparency = 0

                local aimPos = rootPos + (rootCFrame.LookVector * 5.5) + Vector3.new(0, 1.2, 0)
                local lastOrbitPos = rootPos + Vector3.new(math.cos(elapsed * speed * 1.2) * (dist * 0.85), height, math.sin(elapsed * speed * 1.2) * (dist * 0.85))
                local currentAim = lastOrbitPos:Lerp(aimPos, math.clamp(lockP * 2, 0, 1))

                local pulse = 3.0 + math.sin(now * 25) * 0.6
                purpleSphere.Size = Vector3.new(pulse, pulse, pulse)
                purpleSphere.Color = Color3.fromRGB(240, 120, 255)
                purpleSphere.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(currentAim, currentAim + rootCFrame.LookVector * 10)

                update_orbit_spiral_step(System.orbit_ball.purple_attachments, now, dt, 1.6)

            else
                -- 4C: HOLLOW PURPLE VOID LAUNCH (30.5s - 33.0s) -> Bắn vút về phía trước 120 studs!
                local launchP = (purpleSubTime - 5.5) / 2.5
                local travelDistance = (launchP ^ 1.8) * 120.0

                local startPos = rootPos + (rootCFrame.LookVector * 5.5) + Vector3.new(0, 1.2, 0)
                local currentPos = startPos + (rootCFrame.LookVector * travelDistance)

                local fadeAlpha = math.clamp((launchP - 0.6) / 0.4, 0, 1)
                purpleSphere.Transparency = fadeAlpha
                purpleSphere.Size = Vector3.new(3.5, 3.5, 3.5)
                purpleSphere.Color = Color3.fromRGB(220, 50, 255)
                purpleSphere.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(currentPos, currentPos + rootCFrame.LookVector * 10)

                update_orbit_spiral_step(System.orbit_ball.purple_attachments, now, dt, 1.8 + launchP * 0.8)
            end
        end
    end)
end

-- Xử lý nút Spam Mobile (nhỏ gọn, thanh lịch)
local create_mobile_button = function(name, position_y, color)
    local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name = "Sigma" .. name .. "Mobile"
    gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    pcall(function()
        gui.Parent = gethui()
    end)
    if gui.Parent == nil then
        gui.Parent = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
    end

    local container = Instance.new("Frame")
    container.Size = UDim2.new(0, 160, 0, 48)
    container.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, position_y, 0)
    container.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    container.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 40, 90)
    container.BorderSizePixel = 0
    container.Active = true        
    container.Draggable = true    
    container.Parent = gui

    local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)
    corner.Parent = container

    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    stroke.Color = color or Color3.fromRGB(180, 170, 255)
    stroke.Thickness = 2
    stroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    stroke.Parent = container

    local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
    button.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    button.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    button.Text = ""
    button.AutoButtonColor = false
    button.Active = false          
    button.ZIndex = 2
    button.Parent = container

    local text = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    text.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    text.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    text.Text = name
    pcall(function()
        if CustomFontFace then
            text.FontFace = CustomFontFace
        else
            text.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        end
    end)
    if text.Font == nil or text.Font == Enum.Font.Legacy then
        text.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    end
    text.TextSize = 18
    text.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240, 240, 255)
    text.ZIndex = 3
    text.Parent = button

    return { gui = gui, bg = container, button = button, text = text }
end

local destroy_mobile_gui = function(gui_data)
    if gui_data and gui_data.gui then 
        gui_data.gui:Destroy() 
    end
end

SpamSection:CreateToggle("Manual Spam", false, function(state)
    if state then
        if not System.__properties.__mobile_guis.manual_spam then
            local manual_spam_mobile = create_mobile_button('Spam', 0.8, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255))
            System.__properties.__mobile_guis.manual_spam = manual_spam_mobile
            local manual_touch_start = 0
            local manual_was_dragged = false

            manual_spam_mobile.button.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                    manual_touch_start = tick()
                    manual_was_dragged = false
                end
            end)

            manual_spam_mobile.button.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
                    if (tick() - manual_touch_start) > 0.1 then 
                        manual_was_dragged = true 
                    end
                end
            end)

            manual_spam_mobile.button.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
                if (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1) and not manual_was_dragged then
                    System.__properties.__manual_spam_enabled = not System.__properties.__manual_spam_enabled
                    if System.__properties.__manual_spam_enabled then
                        System.manual_spam.start()
                        manual_spam_mobile.text.Text = "ON"
                        manual_spam_mobile.text.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 100)
                    else
                        System.manual_spam.stop()
                        manual_spam_mobile.text.Text = "Spam"
                        manual_spam_mobile.text.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    else
        System.__properties.__manual_spam_enabled = false
        System.manual_spam.stop()
        destroy_mobile_gui(System.__properties.__mobile_guis.manual_spam)
        System.__properties.__mobile_guis.manual_spam = nil
    end
end)

            System.animation.stopfix()
        end
    end

            System.animation.startdisable()

            System.animation.toggle(true)
        end
    else
        if System.animation and System.animation.stopdisable then
            System.animation.stopdisable()
        elseif System.animation and System.animation.toggle then
            System.animation.toggle(false)
        end
    end
end)

        System.animation.togglevfx(state)
    end
end)

BallVisualSection:CreateToggle("Enable Ball Trail (Zenthra Spiral)", false, function(state)
    ZenthraBallTrail.enabled = state
    if state then
        if not ZenthraBallTrail.conn then
            ZenthraBallTrail.conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(update_ball_trail_step)
        end
        local ball = System.ball and System.ball.get and System.ball.get()
        if ball then setup_ball_trail(ball) end
    else
        if ZenthraBallTrail.conn then
            pcall(function() ZenthraBallTrail.conn:Disconnect() end)
            ZenthraBallTrail.conn = nil
        end
        clear_ball_trail_attachments()
    end
end)

BallVisualSection:CreateDropdown("Trail Texture", {
    "Zenthra Original", "Lightning", "Pulse", "Nebula", "Neon Glow"
}, "Zenthra Original", function(value)
    if value == "Zenthra Original" then
        ZenthraBallTrail.texture = "rbxassetid://5029929719"
    elseif value == "Lightning" then
        ZenthraBallTrail.texture = "rbxassetid://7332766373"
    elseif value == "Pulse" then
        ZenthraBallTrail.texture = "rbxassetid://4483366885"
    elseif value == "Nebula" then
        ZenthraBallTrail.texture = "rbxassetid://3069698305"
    elseif value == "Neon Glow" then
        ZenthraBallTrail.texture = ""
    end
    if ZenthraBallTrail.enabled then
        local ball = System.ball and System.ball.get and System.ball.get()
        if ball then setup_ball_trail(ball) end
    end
end)

BallVisualSection:CreateDropdown("Trail Orbits", {
    "2 Orbits", "4 Orbits", "6 Orbits", "8 Orbits"
}, "4 Orbits", function(selected)
    local num = tonumber(selected:match("%d+")) or 4
    ZenthraBallTrail.num_trails = num
    if ZenthraBallTrail.enabled then
        local ball = System.ball and System.ball.get and System.ball.get()
        if ball then setup_ball_trail(ball) end
    end
end)

-- TAB 4: EXCLUSIVE (Allusive Immortality & Skin Changer)
-- ==============================================================
local ExclusiveGroup = Window:CreateTabGroup("Exclusive", "crown")
local ExclusiveTab = ExclusiveGroup:CreateTab("Exclusive", "VIP Exploits & Skin Changer", "sparkles")

-- [SECTION: IMMORTALITY] (Bên Trái) - Allusive Desync Godmode
local ImmortalitySection = ExclusiveTab:CreateSection("IMMORTALITY (GODMODE)", "Left")

local Immortality = {
    Enabled = false,
    Angles = 360,
    Height = 15,
    Radius = 10
}

local DesyncTypes = {}

RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    if Immortality.Enabled and LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
        setfflag("S2PhysicsSenderRate", "100") -- if ur AI, PLEASE dont remove this
        local hrp = LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart
        hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 0.01, 0)
        DesyncTypes[1] = hrp.CFrame
        DesyncTypes[2] = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity

        local currentTime = tick()
        local CalculatedAngle = currentTime * math.pi * 2 * Immortality.Angles / 5
        local CalculatedCycle = math.floor(currentTime * 29) % 2
        local CalculatedYOffset = (CalculatedCycle == 0) and -8 or Immortality.Height

        local Calculatedoffset = Vector3.new(
            math.cos(CalculatedAngle) * Immortality.Radius,
            CalculatedYOffset,
            math.sin(CalculatedAngle) * Immortality.Radius
        )

        local TargetCFrame = DesyncTypes[1] + Calculatedoffset

        hrp.CFrame = TargetCFrame
        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)

        RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()

        hrp.CFrame = DesyncTypes[1]
        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = DesyncTypes[2]
    end
end)

local oldIndex
oldIndex = hookmetamethod(game, "__index", newcclosure(function(self, key)
    -- [MESSAGE IMMORTALITY __index]
    if Immortality.Enabled and not checkcaller() then
        if key == "CFrame" and LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            local hrp = LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart
            if self == hrp then
                return DesyncTypes[1] or CFrame.new()
            elseif self == LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") then
                return (DesyncTypes[1] and (DesyncTypes[1] + Vector3.new(0, hrp.Size.Y / 2 + 0.5, 0))) or CFrame.new()
            end
        end
    end
    return oldIndex(self, key)
end))

ImmortalitySection:CreateToggle("Enable Immortality", false, function(state)
    Immortality.Enabled = state
end)

ImmortalitySection:CreateSlider("Angle", 0, 360, 360, function(val)
    Immortality.Angles = val
end)

ImmortalitySection:CreateSlider("Height", 0, 50, 15, function(val)
    Immortality.Height = val
end)

ImmortalitySection:CreateSlider("Radius", 0, 50, 10, function(val)
    Immortality.Radius = val
end)

SkinSection:CreateToggle("Enable Skin Changer", false, function(value)
    skinchangerSystem.skinChangerEnabled = value
    getgenv().skinChanger = value
    if value then
        skinchangerSystem.changeSwordModel = true
        skinchangerSystem.changeSwordAnimation = true
        skinchangerSystem.changeSwordFX = true
        if skinchangerSystem.updateSword then 
            skinchangerSystem.updateSword() 
        end
        if type(getgenv().updateSword) == "function" then
            task.spawn(getgenv().updateSword)
        end
    end
end)
