    skinchangerSystem.swordModel = text
    skinchangerSystem.swordAnimations = text
    skinchangerSystem.swordFX = text
    getgenv().swordModel = text
    getgenv().swordAnimations = text
    getgenv().swordFX = text
    if skinchangerSystem.skinChangerEnabled and skinchangerSystem.updateSword then
        skinchangerSystem.updateSword()
    end
    if getgenv().skinChanger and type(getgenv().updateSword) == "function" then
        task.spawn(getgenv().updateSword)
    end
end)

-- TAB 3: MISC

local MiscTab = MainGroup:CreateTab("Misc", "Stats, Graphics & Settings", "settings")

-- [SECTION: STATS] (Bên Trái)
local MiscSection = MiscTab:CreateSection("STATS", "Left")

MiscSection:CreateToggle("Ball Stats", false, function(state)
    if state then 
        ball_velocity.start() 
    else 
        ball_velocity.stop() 
    end
end)

-- [SECTION: SETTINGS] (Bên Trái)
local SettingsSection = MiscTab:CreateSection("SETTINGS", "Left")

local Background_Presets = {
    ['None'] = '',
    ['Sora'] = 'https://i.pinimg.com/736x/1f/53/48/1f5348fcbd6f4dfd437356e572d5ba5d.jpg',
    ['Tanya The Evil'] = 'https://i.pinimg.com/1200x/7f/f8/ec/7ff8eca75f2a5695aa082dfe3a3bd5cd.jpg'
}

SettingsSection:CreateDropdown("UI Background", {"None", "Sora", "Tanya The Evil"}, "None", function(selected)
    local url = Background_Presets[selected]
    if url and Window then
        Window:SetBackgroundImage(url)
    end
end)

SettingsSection:CreateDropdown("Image Scale", {"Crop", "Fit", "Stretch"}, "Crop", function(selected)
    if Window then
        Window:SetBackgroundScaleType(selected)
    end
end)


local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local CONFIG_FILE_NAME = "Altheris.json"

local function save_current_config()
    local configData = {
        properties = {
            __autoparry_enabled = System.__properties.__autoparry_enabled,
            __accuracy = System.__properties.__accuracy,
            __randomize_accuracy = System.__properties.__randomize_accuracy,
            __random_accuracy_min = System.__properties.__random_accuracy_min,
            __random_accuracy_max = System.__properties.__random_accuracy_max,
            __parry_range_multiplier = System.__properties.__parry_range_multiplier or 1.0,
            __curve_sensitivity = System.__properties.__curve_sensitivity,
            __auto_spam_enabled = System.__properties.__auto_spam_enabled,
            __spam_threshold = System.__properties.__spam_threshold,
            __manual_spam_enabled = System.__properties.__manual_spam_enabled,
        },
        detections = {
            __infinity = System.__config.__detections.__infinity,
            __deathslash = System.__config.__detections.__deathslash,
            __timehole = System.__config.__detections.__timehole,
            __slashesoffury = System.__config.__detections.__slashesoffury,
            __phantom = System.__config.__detections.__phantom,
            __forcefield = System.__config.__detections.__forcefield,
            __pulsed = System.__config.__detections.__pulsed,
            __aerodynamic = System.__config.__detections.__aerodynamic,
            __combocounter = System.__config.__detections.__combocounter,
            __dribble = System.__config.__detections.__dribble,
        },
        spam = {
            rps = getgenv().SpammingRPS or 480,
            burst_packets = getgenv().ManualPackets or 7,
            mode = getgenv().AutoSpamMode or "Remote",
        },
        visuals = {
            visualizer_enabled = System.visualizer and System.visualizer.enabled or false,
            trail_enabled = ZenthraBallTrail and ZenthraBallTrail.enabled or false,
            orbit_enabled = System.orbit_ball and System.orbit_ball.enabled or false,
            ability_esp = System.__properties and System.__properties.__ability_esp_enabled or false,
        },
        fov = {
            enabled = getgenv().CameraEnabled or false,
            fov = getgenv().CameraFOV or 70,
        },
        triggerbot = {
            enabled = System.triggerbot and System.triggerbot.enabled or false,
            delay_ms = System.triggerbot and System.triggerbot.delay_ms or 0,
        }
    }

    local success, encoded = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONEncode(configData) end)
    if success and encoded then
        local write_ok = pcall(function() writefile(CONFIG_FILE_NAME, encoded) end)
        if write_ok then
            if Window and Window.Notify then
                Window.Notify("Config", "Đã lưu cấu hình vào máy thành công!", 3)
            end
        else
            if Window and Window.Notify then
                Window.Notify("Config", "Không thể ghi file cấu hình!", 3)
            end
        end
    end
end

local function load_saved_config(is_autoload)
    local read_ok, content = pcall(function() return readfile(CONFIG_FILE_NAME) end)
    if not (read_ok and content and #content > 0) then
        return false
    end

    local dec_ok, decoded = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(content) end)
    if not (dec_ok and type(decoded) == "table") then
        return false
    end

    local ui = Window and Window.Elements

    -- 1. Properties
    if decoded.properties then
        for k, v in pairs(decoded.properties) do
            System.__properties[k] = v
        end
        if ui then
            if decoded.properties.__autoparry_enabled ~= nil and ui["Auto Parry"] and ui["Auto Parry"].Set then
                pcall(function() ui["Auto Parry"]:Set(decoded.properties.__autoparry_enabled) end)
            end
            if decoded.properties.__accuracy and ui["Parry Accuracy (%)"] and ui["Parry Accuracy (%)"].Set then
                pcall(function() ui["Parry Accuracy (%)"]:Set(decoded.properties.__accuracy) end)
            end
            if decoded.properties.__parry_range_multiplier and ui["Parry Range Multiplier"] and ui["Parry Range Multiplier"].Set then
                pcall(function() ui["Parry Range Multiplier"]:Set(decoded.properties.__parry_range_multiplier) end)
            end
            if decoded.properties.__randomize_accuracy ~= nil and ui["Randomize Accuracy (Legit)"] and ui["Randomize Accuracy (Legit)"].Set then
                pcall(function() ui["Randomize Accuracy (Legit)"]:Set(decoded.properties.__randomize_accuracy) end)
            end
            if decoded.properties.__random_accuracy_min and ui["Random Accuracy Min (%)"] and ui["Random Accuracy Min (%)"].Set then
                pcall(function() ui["Random Accuracy Min (%)"]:Set(decoded.properties.__random_accuracy_min) end)
            end
            if decoded.properties.__random_accuracy_max and ui["Random Accuracy Max (%)"] and ui["Random Accuracy Max (%)"].Set then
                pcall(function() ui["Random Accuracy Max (%)"]:Set(decoded.properties.__random_accuracy_max) end)
            end
            if decoded.properties.__curve_sensitivity and ui["Curve Sensitivity (%)"] and ui["Curve Sensitivity (%)"].Set then
                pcall(function() ui["Curve Sensitivity (%)"]:Set(decoded.properties.__curve_sensitivity) end)
            end
            if decoded.properties.__auto_spam_enabled ~= nil and ui["Auto Spam"] and ui["Auto Spam"].Set then
                pcall(function() ui["Auto Spam"]:Set(decoded.properties.__auto_spam_enabled) end)
            end
            if decoded.properties.__spam_threshold and ui["Spam Threshold (Combo)"] and ui["Spam Threshold (Combo)"].Set then
                pcall(function() ui["Spam Threshold (Combo)"]:Set(decoded.properties.__spam_threshold) end)
            end
            if decoded.properties.__manual_spam_enabled ~= nil and ui["Manual Spam"] and ui["Manual Spam"].Set then
                pcall(function() ui["Manual Spam"]:Set(decoded.properties.__manual_spam_enabled) end)
            end
        end
    end

    -- 2. Detections
    if decoded.detections then
        for k, v in pairs(decoded.detections) do
            System.__config.__detections[k] = v
        end
        if ui then
            local det_map = {
                __infinity = "Infinity Detection",
                __deathslash = "Death Slash Detection",
                __timehole = "Time Hole Detection",
                __slashesoffury = "Slashes of Fury Detection",
                __phantom = "Phantom Detection",
                __forcefield = "Forcefield Detection",
                __pulsed = "Pulsed Ball Filter",
                __aerodynamic = "Aerodynamic Slash + Tornado",
                __combocounter = "Combo Counter Filter",
                __dribble = "Dribble Ball Filter"
            }
            for k, ui_name in pairs(det_map) do
                if decoded.detections[k] ~= nil and ui[ui_name] and ui[ui_name].Set then
                    pcall(function() ui[ui_name]:Set(decoded.detections[k]) end)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- 3. Spam settings
    if decoded.spam then
        if decoded.spam.rps then
            getgenv().SpammingRPS = decoded.spam.rps
            if ui and ui["Spamming RPS"] and ui["Spamming RPS"].Set then
                pcall(function() ui["Spamming RPS"]:Set(decoded.spam.rps) end)
            end
        end
        if decoded.spam.burst_packets then
            getgenv().ManualPackets = decoded.spam.burst_packets
            if System.config then System.config.manual_packets = decoded.spam.burst_packets end
            if ui and ui["Burst Packets"] and ui["Burst Packets"].Set then
                pcall(function() ui["Burst Packets"]:Set(decoded.spam.burst_packets) end)
            end
        end
        if decoded.spam.mode then
            getgenv().AutoSpamMode = decoded.spam.mode
        end
    end

    -- 4. Visuals
    if decoded.visuals then
        if decoded.visuals.visualizer_enabled ~= nil then
            System.visualizer.enabled = decoded.visuals.visualizer_enabled
            if ui and ui["Enable 3D Visualizer"] and ui["Enable 3D Visualizer"].Set then
                pcall(function() ui["Enable 3D Visualizer"]:Set(decoded.visuals.visualizer_enabled) end)
            end
            if decoded.visuals.visualizer_enabled then
                update_visualizer_ring()
            else
                destroy_visualizer_parts()
            end
        end
        if decoded.visuals.trail_enabled ~= nil and ui and ui["Enable Ball Trail (Zenthra Spiral)"] and ui["Enable Ball Trail (Zenthra Spiral)"].Set then
            pcall(function() ui["Enable Ball Trail (Zenthra Spiral)"]:Set(decoded.visuals.trail_enabled) end)
        end
        if decoded.visuals.orbit_enabled ~= nil and ui and ui["Enable Orbit Ball"] and ui["Enable Orbit Ball"].Set then
            pcall(function() ui["Enable Orbit Ball"]:Set(decoded.visuals.orbit_enabled) end)
        end
        if decoded.visuals.ability_esp ~= nil and ui and ui["Ability ESP"] and ui["Ability ESP"].Set then
            pcall(function() ui["Ability ESP"]:Set(decoded.visuals.ability_esp) end)
        end
    end

    -- 5. FOV
    if decoded.fov then
        if decoded.fov.enabled ~= nil and ui and ui["Enable Custom FOV"] and ui["Enable Custom FOV"].Set then
            pcall(function() ui["Enable Custom FOV"]:Set(decoded.fov.enabled) end)
        end
        if decoded.fov.fov and ui and ui["Camera FOV"] and ui["Camera FOV"].Set then
            pcall(function() ui["Camera FOV"]:Set(decoded.fov.fov) end)
        end
    end

    -- 6. Triggerbot
    if decoded.triggerbot then
        if decoded.triggerbot.enabled ~= nil and ui and ui["Triggerbot (Zenthra)"] and ui["Triggerbot (Zenthra)"].Set then
            pcall(function() ui["Triggerbot (Zenthra)"]:Set(decoded.triggerbot.enabled) end)
        end
        if decoded.triggerbot.delay_ms and ui and ui["Triggerbot Delay (ms)"] and ui["Triggerbot Delay (ms)"].Set then
            pcall(function() ui["Triggerbot Delay (ms)"]:Set(decoded.triggerbot.delay_ms) end)
        end
    end

    -- 7. Startup active systems
    if System.__properties.__autoparry_enabled then
        System.autoparry.start()
    end
    if System.__properties.__auto_spam_enabled then
        System.auto_spam.start()
    end

    if is_autoload then
        if Window and Window.Notify then
            Window.Notify("Config", "Đã tự động nạp cấu hình đã lưu!", 4)
        end
    else
        if Window and Window.Notify then
            Window.Notify("Config", "Đã nạp cấu hình thành công!", 3)
        end
    end

    return true
end

SettingsSection:CreateButton("Save Config", function()
    save_current_config()
end)

SettingsSection:CreateButton("Owner Config", function()
    -- Core toggles
    System.__properties.__autoparry_enabled = true
    System.__properties.__accuracy = 100
    System.__properties.__parry_range_multiplier = 1.0
    System.__properties.__randomize_accuracy = true
    System.__properties.__random_accuracy_min = 70
    System.__properties.__random_accuracy_max = 95
    System.__properties.__curve_sensitivity = 70
    
    -- Triggerbot
    if System.triggerbot then
        System.triggerbot.enabled = true
        System.triggerbot.delay_ms = 15
        System.triggerbot.start()
    end
    
    -- Spam Config (480 RPS, Burst 7, Remote)
    System.__properties.__auto_spam_enabled = true
    System.__properties.__spam_threshold = 3
    getgenv().SpammingRPS = 480
    getgenv().ManualPackets = 7
    getgenv().AutoSpamMode = "Remote"
    if not System.config then System.config = {} end
    System.config.manual_packets = 7

    -- 3D Parry Visualizer ON
    System.visualizer.enabled = true
    update_visualizer_ring()
    
    -- Detections
    System.__config.__detections.__infinity = true
    System.__config.__detections.__phantom = true
    System.__config.__detections.__forcefield = true
    System.__config.__detections.__deathslash = true
    System.__config.__detections.__timehole = true
    System.__config.__detections.__slashesoffury = true
    System.__config.__detections.__aerodynamic = true
    System.__config.__detections.__pulsed = true
    System.__config.__detections.__dribble = true
    System.__config.__detections.__combocounter = true
    
    if Window and Window.Notify then Window.Notify("Config", "Owner Config Loaded: 480 RPS, Burst 7, 3D Visualizer & All Detections ON!", 4) end
end)
-- [SECTION: FOV] (Bên Phải)
local FOVSection = MiscTab:CreateSection("FOV", "Right")

FOVSection:CreateToggle("Enable Custom FOV", false, function(value)
    getgenv().CameraEnabled = value
    local Camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

    if value then
        getgenv().CameraFOV = getgenv().CameraFOV or 70
        Camera.FieldOfView = getgenv().CameraFOV

        if not getgenv().FOVLoop then
            getgenv().FOVLoop = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                if getgenv().CameraEnabled then
                    workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView = getgenv().CameraFOV
                end
            end)
        end
    else
        Camera.FieldOfView = 70
        if getgenv().FOVLoop then
            getgenv().FOVLoop:Disconnect()
            getgenv().FOVLoop = nil
        end
    end
end)

FOVSection:CreateSlider("Camera FOV", 50, 120, 70, function(value)
    getgenv().CameraFOV = value
    if getgenv().CameraEnabled then
        workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView = value
    end
end)

-- [SECTION: GRAPHICS] (Bên Phải)
local GraphicsSection = MiscTab:CreateSection("GRAPHICS", "Right")

GraphicsSection:CreateToggle("Potato Graphics", false, function(state)
    if System and System.graphics and System.graphics.enablepotato then
        System.graphics.enablepotato(state)
    end
end)

GraphicsSection:CreateToggle("Night Mode", false, function(state)
    if System and System.graphics and System.graphics.enablenightmode then
        System.graphics.enablenightmode(state)
    end
end)

local SecuritySection = MiscTab:CreateSection("SECURITY", "Right")
SecuritySection:CreateToggle("Staff/Mod Detector", false, function(state)
    if System and System.security and System.security.staff_detector then
        System.security.staff_detector(state)
    end
end)



System.security = System.security or {}
System.security.staff_detector = function(state)
    local properties = getProperties()
    if not properties then return end
    properties.connections = properties.connections or {}
    
    if state then
        if properties.connections.__staff_det then return end
        
        local function checkPlayer(player)
            -- Check known staff groups or typical admin markers (Blade ball group id: 13732323)
            -- For safety, any player with "Dev" or "Mod" in their name, or high rank in creator group
            task.spawn(function()
                local ok, rank = pcall(function() return player:GetRankInGroup(13732323) end)
                if ok and rank and rank >= 200 then
                    Window.Notify("SECURITY ALERT", "A Blade Ball Staff Member (" .. player.Name .. ") is in the game!", 10)
                    -- Auto disable parry for safety if a staff is detected
                    if System.__properties.__autoparry_enabled then
                        System.autoparry.stop()
                        Window.Notify("SECURITY", "Auto Parry has been temporarily disabled for your safety.", 5)
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
        
        for _, p in ipairs(game.Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= LocalPlayer then checkPlayer(p) end
        end
        
        properties.connections.__staff_det = game.Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(checkPlayer)
    else
        if properties.connections.__staff_det then
            properties.connections.__staff_det:Disconnect()
            properties.connections.__staff_det = nil
        end
    end
end

-- ==============================================================
-- FINALIZATION & NOTIFICATIONS
-- ==============================================================

-- Auto-load saved config if exists, otherwise initialize defaults
task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(0.2)
    local loaded = load_saved_config(true)
    if not loaded and System.visualizer.enabled then
        task.wait(0.3)
        update_visualizer_ring()
    end
end)

Window.Notify("Altheris", "Successfully loaded Blade Ball!", 5)
Window.Notify("System", "System initialized successfully", 5)

-- Trả về biến Library ra biến toàn cục nếu các script khác cần dùng
getgenv().Library = Window
